# Current Formal Frontier Toward Proving or Disproving the Riemann Hypothesis

## Formal research map of HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower

**Corpus cutoff:** 4 August 2026, committed Lean snapshot
`af23c0451d8ed289615213e89771d17cf912bcd7`. “Formal corpus” means declarations reachable from the
three area umbrellas at that snapshot; unwired or uncommitted work-in-progress files are not counted
as established frontier results. Later paper-only results are labeled provisionally rather than
silently promoted to formal closure.

## Abstract

This paper maps the exact formal frontier toward proving or disproving the Riemann hypothesis in
`HTGeometry`, `HilbertMetric`, and `RHTower`. It treats Lean declaration types as authority and
quotients thousands of criteria by their first unresolved source premise. It records each route's
endpoint, proved chain, obstruction, open core, dead mechanisms, and strongest positive survivor.

The corpus proves neither `RiemannHypothesis` nor its negation. It proves many exact RH
equivalences, completed connectors, and no-go classifications. Route A is terminalized: fixed
source-faithful physical-phase transports are identity, nonidentity transports have an explicit
phase-connection defect, and arbitrary aperture-dependent positive Markov orientation is feasible
exactly when the original source scalar is nonnegative; universal completed feasibility is RH.
Routes B, C2, D, F, G2, H, I, J, and K are retired as independent mechanisms. Route M is
terminalized at an explicit source-faithful reader whose remaining flatness statement is exactly
RH. The live independent routes are C1, D2, E, G, L, N, O, P, Q, and R. A separate unlettered
CCM four-unit source-codiagonal lane is also live; it is neither Route A nor the C1 class-space
branch. Route S is retained as an upstream algebraic/dynamical program.

Source formation is substantially complete. Wold activation gives `ζ(s)/ζ(s+h)` and first
normal jet `-ζ'/ζ`; its squarefree diagonal disintegrates into Bernoulli occupancy and geometric
valuation depth. Möbius depth differences, Fisher damping, Gamma whitening, primitive
von-Mangoldt promotion, completed Gamma correction, and source-to-Xi Hilbert/Krein landing are
formalized. The primitive promotion is unbounded in valuation depth, and reflected exponent
sectors agree only on `Re(s)=1/2`. The remaining problem is not another scalar criterion: it is
an unconditional arithmetic operation acting before source labels, depth, and reflection
orientation are erased.

Route E remains open after a broad local closure. Coprime opposite-sign factor pairs and
prime-anchor reciprocal-marker Hall capacity exist in every sufficiently long interval; fixed
off-normal Wold breadth deviations are transported; and every fixed finite polynomial moment
family in `ω(n)` is Möbius-parity blind. The formal sieve layer supplies interval Bonferroni and
Selberg bounds, explicit Mertens prime-sum estimates, Hardy--Ramanujan tail machinery, and
large-sieve/Farey infrastructure. It now also gives a sharp dyadic reciprocal-prime window bound
and an absolute uniform bound for `∑_{p>z}p^{-(1+1/log z)}` over every finite truncation. None
supplies the source-predeclared physical adjacency now required at the central normal-breadth
face. The immediate open object is an explicit relation `R_X` on squarefree ordinary integers,
fixed independently of partial-sum imbalance, together with one joint interval-Hall and
co-moving-cutoff flux theorem for that relation. Exceptional Carleson is a specialization of this
full interval expansion, not a separate open mechanism.

Route J closes negatively as an independent Julia-energy mechanism. Uniform unrenormalized
coherent energy is impossible, bounded polarizations have insufficient capacity, post hoc
diagonal subtraction destroys one-node capacity, and the unrestricted residual capacity is
exactly RH. The normalized joint prime-depth incidence `exp(a(λ_j-λ_i))` is also a vertex-gauge
coboundary: every cycle holonomy is one and every finite determinant or trace moment is
horizontally blind. The strongest positive survivor is not an unfinished Route-J lemma: it is the
design requirement for a new principal-relative current defined before good/bad projection,
genuinely nonseparable and non-coboundary. The existing singular joint-depth/Ward realization is
owned by Route R; any different realization would constitute an additional route.

Route L has a concrete relative cohomological detector. Centered-flow countertransport flattens
absolute curvature, while raw-minus-flat relative curvature retains the Deninger mate
polarization. Finite Tate shifts realize it as `(1-χ_s(n))β_m`; reflection gives
`K_n(s)β_m`, and the top-coordinate reader and completed finite-support Xi incidence recover
that coefficient exactly. Meyer and Calderón--Witt comparisons prove non-erasure, not vanishing.
The completed Xi Green operator and nonzero primitive, coefficient-faithful Green-to-
Calderón--Witt incidence, ordinary-integer BV boundary, actual Burnol/Deninger conormal, and strict
resolved Route-R chain provenance are constructed. A literal rational-adelic degree-one cyclic-bar
source object now also maps before divisor specialization to the ordered physical two-leg landing; its
ordinary-prime tensor atom has trace one, multiplicity remains filtered, and the physical paracyclic
defect has exact residue `(delta_n(rho)-1)c_rho`. The open core is therefore no longer “construct
some chain map” or even “construct a pre-divisor physical comparison.” It is the source theorem
making this measured physical defect null-homologous in the literal arithmetic cyclic complex—or
equivalently the singular boundary side of the strict Green--BV comparison whose fixed physical
reader kills target boundaries—without erasing the prime detector or assuming `K_n(s)=0`.

Route R's source, joint-depth Hilbert/Krein carrier, Gamma completion, and regular and singular
source-to-Xi landings are closed. Positive or nonnegative Gamma shorting cannot annihilate an
off-line displacement. Neither unordered scalar summation nor scalar distributional completion
can evade isolated prime-depth frequency uniqueness. Finite clock/incidence expressions are
gauge-conjugate to zero displacement and spectrally blind. Route R now escapes that closed class:
the source records `m_(p,e)=(1-p⁻¹)p⁻ᵉ` and `λ_(p,e)=log(p^(e+1))` define the alternating
connection `θ(i,j)=m_iλ_j-m_jλ_i`. Lean proves exact triangle curvature `-log(2)/8`, no vertex
potential, and no representation as a function of clock difference. Its divisor-resolved
covariant square vanishes globally exactly when RH holds. The crossed graded-`det2` anomaly now
has an unconditional reflection-doubled Cartier trivialization and preserves a hyperbolic Stokes
pairing for every centered rate. Lean also proves this structure is nonselective: preservation of
its positive same-fibre diagonal is equivalent pointwise to the critical line and globally to RH.
Route R's positive resolved polarization and its Stokes flatness are now constructed, as are a
genuine rational-adelic prime-isotropy class and a strict prime/depth/divisor-faithful chain map to
the resolved actual-Xi complex. An independent bilateral prime-orbit Stokes regulator annihilates
every relative source boundary, descends to genuine `H¹`, and takes value one on the retained
class. The class lands nontrivially as a resolved target boundary and is annihilated by the next
differential. This closes source provenance, non-erasure, and source Stokes period at that grade,
not scalar physical descent. The exact open core is a graded variable-ideal, coefficient-faithful singular
Cartier comparison identifying that resolved boundary with the fixed same-fibre physical fibre on
the actual leading Xi conormal. Fixed Hilbert/Schatten completion cannot carry both reflected
essential-edge sectors throughout the full critical strip. Ordinary Cartier exactness,
determinant-one reflection balance, indefinite Stokes conservation, transported positivity, and
bare associated-grade preservation are all constructed and explicitly nonselective.

CCM now supplies three distinct advances. Its exact closed nonunital strong cyclic quotient,
descended Lefschetz action, Real structure, Clifford transgression, and nonzero Haar--Mellin
separator give a concrete class-space branch of Route C1. The untwisted Lefschetz operator is
Real-even and cannot equal the required anti-Real orientation character; multiplication by `i` is
the canonical surviving anti-Real transgression. Separately, the rational-adelic prime-isotropy
mapping cone feeds Route R as above. Third, the squarefree Fisher lower cutoff corner has exact
critical energy four, the critical source escapes every fixed compact synthesis, and every
internal Xi jet has a source-defined square-summable physical horizon. This leaves one precise
prelimit, cofinal, noncompact Gamma/Sonin/Hodge incidence problem to the continued Xi Ward column.
None of these results proves the missing Weil trace-pairing positivity, singular Cartier
comparison, or Ward incidence.

Route O has moved past seed construction at paper level. The literal radial prolate equation has
an exact Liouville normal form in which aperture dependence cancels from the comparison defect,
the apparent double pole cancels to a simple pole, and Lean proves the uniform bound
`|a(x)| ≤ 40/(x²-1)` for `0 ≤ s ≤ 1/2`, `x>1`, the finite-mass bound
`∫_A^B |a|/ξ' ≤ 127`, and the resulting Volterra exponent `≤254 C²/(cW)`. A provisional
Stage-2 paper proof now closes the complete radial-prime response, Poisson center,
critical-Mellin coupling, polar contribution, and exterior seed law. The concrete radial-PSWF
realization still needs hostile audit and Lean. The scientific open core is no longer this source
assembly: it is source-derived profile exhaustion and reduced-resolvent control of every possible
restricted-Weil ground direction.

The disproof frontier is exact but empty: one certified off-line Xi zero, or any of several
equivalent finite negative witnesses, would disprove RH. The most source-explicit current candidate
is a growing-order squared-Xi reverse-Bessel radial jet; all formalized orders through eighteen are
positive, and the surviving `n≥19` comparison is unproved.

`RH equivalent` means a complete target, not progress toward its source proof. `Open core`
means the first unproved premise after proved connectors are removed. A dead mechanism kills only
its stated construction; its strongest nontrivial survivor remains in this map.

## 1. Current conclusion

The corpus has solved most connector problems and has proved neither RH nor its negation. Source
formation, primitive prime-depth resolution, completed Gamma correction, and source-to-Xi landing
are no longer the common gap. The common gap is an unconditional arithmetic annihilation or
orientation theorem that acts before bounded codiagonalization, vertex-gauge reduction, or
frequency separation erases the horizontal source defect.

The shortest current chains are:

- Route R: construct a graded variable-ideal, coefficient-faithful singular Cartier comparison
  carrying the retained rational-adelic prime-isotropy boundary from the positive resolved frame
  to the fixed same-fibre physical Xi conormal; transported positivity, Stokes flatness, strict
  resolved chain provenance, and the source-pure norm-conservative selection theorem already hold;
- Route L: prove source-null-homology of the now explicitly measured physical paracyclic defect,
  equivalently construct the singular boundary side of the Green--BV square so the existing
  Burnol/Deninger reader annihilates it without erasing the ordinary-prime detector;
- Route E: define an explicit source-predeclared physical relation `R_X` on the squarefree central
  normal Wold face, then prove its joint interval-Hall expansion and co-moving zero/one-large-prime
  cutoff flux at square-root scale;
- Route C1: either construct the singular Hedenmalm common-domain/boundary transgression that
  realizes the already identified source Weil form faithfully without assuming its sign, or
  construct and identify the positive bivariant/intersection trace pairing on the exact closed
  CCM quotient;
- CCM four-unit lane: construct the finite-parameter, cofinal noncompact incidence carrying the
  joined one-sided Fisher corner and admitted all-jet horizon to the continued Xi Ward column
  without increasing its exact four-unit energy;
- Routes D2, G, N, O, P, and Q: supply their stated high-frequency, meromorphic producer,
  prime-normal, whole-ground profile-exhaustion, compact moment, or saturation theorem. Route O's
  complete exterior seed law is provisionally closed on paper; its concrete PSWF realization is
  not yet Lean-certified and its full restricted-Weil ground selection remains open.

Sharp dyadic Route I, Julia-energy Route J, and quadratic Route K are not live alternatives:
Route I collides exactly with retired Route C2, Route J closes at an unbounded coherent-energy
floor and an RH-equivalent unrestricted residual, and Route K's all-order continuation is Route P.
Route A remains a useful exact endpoint but no independent positive-factorization mechanism
survives. Route M remains a terminalized interface, not an RH proof.

### Cross-route merger verdict — a parallel polarized source atlas, not a serial super-route

The corpus does support a merged architecture for C1, D2, E, G, L, N, O, P, Q, R, and CCM-FU,
but it does not support concatenating their terminal RH criteria. The proved merger law is
parallel and source-pointed.

Fix the actual resolved Green defect `g_(p,q)` at a prime `p` and Xi divisor copy `q`; its
coefficient formula gives

```text
g_(p,q)=0 iff Re(rho_q)=1/2.
```

For each route chart `j`, construct a source comparison `Phi_(j,p,q)`, an admitted sector
`S_(j,q)`, and an observation `O_(j,q)` conservative on that sector. If

```text
Phi_j(g_(p,q)) in S_j,
O_j(Phi_j(g_(p,q)))=0
```

for every selected chart, and the parallel record is jointly faithful at zero,

```text
(forall j, Phi_j(g_(p,q))=0) -> g_(p,q)=0,
```

then routewise conservativity kills every image, joint faithfulness kills `g_(p,q)`, and RH
follows when this holds at every divisor copy. No single chart must be faithful. The mathematical
content is the intersection of route radicals; serial postprocessing can only enlarge them.

**2026-08-04 refinement — quotient route charts by source isogeny before taking the parallel
record.**  Route labels do not imply radical independence.  The newly proved all-prime transition
law identifies Route R's independently constructed graded-`det₂` triangle anomaly `A_p(s)` with
Route L's mate monodromy `delta_p(s)` by

```text
Area_p = -(1-p^(-1))^3 log p,
A_p(s)^(p^3) = delta_p(s)^((p-1)^3).
```

At `p=2`, `A_2(s)^8=delta_2(s)`.  Moreover the actual CCM cofinal source-homology readout is

```text
(delta_2(s)-1) * jet * trace
= (A_2(s)-1)(1+A_2(s)+...+A_2(s)^7) * jet * trace,
```

and the second factor is strictly positive.  Therefore Route R curvature correction,
graded-`det₂` counterboundary, Route L paracyclic monodromy, and CCM cofinal Bockstein form one
source-isogeny class with the same zero radical.  Parallelizing them repeats one detector; it does
not reduce the intersection.  This law is formalized in
`RHTower/PrimeDepthBVWoldSourceDaggerAtlasTransition.lean`.

The same law persists through every Wold depth:

```text
Area_(p,e)=-(1-p^(-1))^3 p^(-e) log p,
A_(p,e+1)^p=A_(p,e),
A_(p,e+k)^(p^k)=A_(p,e),
A_(p,e)^(p^(e+3))=delta_p^((p-1)^3).
```

So Route R's depth tower is an unconditional coherent Kummer tower of scalar anomalies and every
level has the same radical.  Depth adds roots, not a new atlas coordinate.  This is not the
divisor-bearing holomorphic Xi-root tower of `RiemannTower.lean`; confusing those towers would
turn Route A's exact multiplicity obstruction into a tautological scalar root construction.
It also cannot supply the non-scalar class conjectured by
`DAC_FROBENIUS_COHERENCE_DEFECT_SPECTRUM`: every same-prime finite power-associator vanishes
identically.  A transverse Frobenius class must retain the failure of local homotopies to glue
across source charts, not merely their scalar anomaly values.

The corrected atlas census is:

- one **horizontal-character class** contains Route R's nonseparable prime/depth anomaly and
  graded correction, Route L's physical Burnol/Deninger monodromy, and CCM's literal cyclic source
  Bockstein;
- Route R still supplies coefficient-faithful resolved Green provenance and positive transported
  frame not present in the scalar transition, while Route L supplies the physical reader and
  cyclic-bar source; these are complementary infrastructure, not independent vanishing radicals;
- CCM-FU supplies a four-unit, pre-scalar Ward chart whose source escapes every fixed compact
  synthesis;
- C1 supplies singular Hedenmalm and exact CCM class-space Hodge/intersection candidates;
- E, D2, and G supply breadth, continued-frequency, and meromorphic-phase charts;
- N and O supply prime-normal and whole-ground completion/profile charts; and
- P and Q supply the completed moment and theta-boundary charts.

Most latter charts do not yet have a comparison satisfying the displayed common-test identities;
their route-specific open cores are exactly the missing admission or annihilation theorem. Thus
the merger is a genuine new opening but not a completed new proof route. It changes the search in
one important way: one need not close every route, nor make one route globally faithful. A small
parallel family may suffice if its **primitive source-isogeny classes**, rather than nominal route
labels, have common radical excluding the horizontal Green defect.

Across the routes, the remaining obstruction has one fundamental mathematical nature:

> construct a source-derived, coefficient-faithful, reflection-odd relation that survives a
> singular/cofinal/noncompact limit and either annihilates the resolved horizontal defect in its
> own grade or makes the intersection of all surviving route radicals trivial on that defect.

This explains the recurring walls. Seed existence is weaker than whole-space exhaustion (O);
positive or scalar completion loses orientation (C1, P, Q, R); fixed compact synthesis loses
escaping source mass (CCM-FU); ordinary completion or finite Rees order loses the decisive domain
(C1, N, R); local/finite data do not control the cofinal tail (D2, E, G); and cancellation across
different grades is forbidden (L, R). The shared open problem is selection under singular source
descent, not representation of Xi, construction of another positive consumer, or another RH
equivalence.

## 2. What the foundational tower actually proves

`RHTower/RiemannTower.lean` supplies the logical skeleton:

```text
RH
↔ Xi has no zero in Re(s) > 1/2
↔ a coherent dyadic Kummer root tower exists
↔ every right-half zero multiplicity is divisible by every power of two
↔ analytic 2^k-roots exist on the right half-plane for every k.
```

`RHCharacterizations.rh_tfae` packages fifteen equivalent coordinates: right-half
nonvanishing, tower existence, all ladders, empty off-line height locus, top valuation
profile, zero census jumps, empty boxes, zero-free descent, trivial deck charge, line
holography, window certificates, and zero local modulus slopes.

These declarations are exact and valuable. They are not source producers. A proof route
must establish one coordinate from information that does not already contain Xi zero
location. The same rule applies to the hundreds of later `↔ RiemannHypothesis`
theorems.

### Corpus-native route space beyond classical templates

The formal corpus does not merely repackage classical RH criteria. Its no-go theorems force a
route-design space whose primitives are not scalar Dirichlet estimates, positive operators,
zero-fed spectra, or determinant limits:

| Corpus-forced primitive | Formal evidence | Present status |
|---|---|---|
| pre-projection principal-relative current | Route-J energy divergence, diagonal-retention, and endpoint-completeness theorems exclude every advertised post-projection capacity repair | positive survivor of retired J; a new route if constructed |
| nonseparable prime/depth--divisor arithmetic differential | `θ(i,j)=m_iλ_j-m_jλ_i` has exact curvature `-log(2)/8`, no vertex potential, and no clock-difference representation; crossed BV/clock occupation legs produce an independent equal-grade graded-`det2` counterboundary; resolved frames carry a positive definite parallel Hermitian metric; its fixed-source excess is the exact square `a²θ(i,j)²|c|²/(λ_i²λ_j²)` | constructed Route R corrected square, positive resolved Stokes polarization, and sharp fixed-Cartier descent obstruction; source rigidity of the two metrics remains open |
| source-first chain descent rather than post hoc cohomology | the ordinary Wold/BV Hochschild boundary exists, but physical landing reads exactly the nonzero Burnol/Deninger radial defect | live Route L open core |
| prelimit noncompact one-corner incidence | the lower Fisher cutoff corner carries exactly four critical energy units, the critical channel converges weakly but not strongly, every fixed compact synthesis kills it, and every internal Xi jet has a physical `ell2` horizon | live unlettered CCM-FU lane; construct the moving Gamma/Sonin/Hodge incidence and identify its Ward column |
| exact closed CCM class-space quotient with Real/Clifford structure | the literal nonunital strong cyclic target, closed restriction quotient, descended invertible Lefschetz action, even Real structure, nonzero Haar--Mellin separator, exact Real-parity no-go, and canonical anti-Real `iL` survivor are constructed | concrete C1-CCM carrier; bivariant/intersection pairing, Hodge sign, and Weil-form identification remain open |
| moving, all-order, provenance-preserving carrier | finite Möbius/Wold moments are parity-blind and fixed finite selectors are saturated | live Routes E and P at distinct source laws |
| source algebraicity or divisor-preserving dynamics | HTGeometry supplies independent rigidity and transversality but no Xi-source handoff | upstream Route S; not yet a proof route |

This table mixes constructed routes with exact design constraints. Route R's differential,
independent equal-grade corrected square, reflection-hyperbolic Cartier trivialization, and
positive resolved Hermitian polarization with unconditional Stokes parallelism exist in Lean.
So do its genuine prime zero-isotropy relative complex, retained ordinary-prime class, and strict
prime/depth/divisor-faithful comparison to the resolved actual-Xi complex. Its bilateral
prime-orbit Stokes regulator descends to source `H¹`, kills every relative boundary, and detects
the retained class with value one.
The fixed source/Cartier metric differs on the literal vertical conormal by one explicit
nonnegative BV--Wold square; global vanishing of that square is RH. What does not exist is source
rigidity identifying the transported metric field with that fixed same-fibre metric. Route L
likewise has an exact tail to RH but not its strict
descent. The post-J pre-projection current is only a falsifiable successor class until a literal
source object and unconditional handoff are built. Its construction must be recorded as a new
route unless it is literally Route R, not used to reopen J. The ambient discrete-connection
formalism is classical; the exact BV--Wold arithmetic coupling is recorded by the corpus as new
scientific knowledge after internal and external collision audits. That scoped result does not
turn absence of a located collision into an unrestricted historical-priority claim.

### Route A — literal CCM first clock `[TERMINALIZED]`

**Status.** `RETIRED AS AN INDEPENDENT SOURCE-PRODUCING MECHANISM / RETAINED AS AN EXACT RH
COORDINATE`. This does not assert the first-clock sign or RH.

**Endpoint.**

- `CCMFirstClockPublishedSourceEventuallyBoundedBelow`;
- `CCMFirstClockScaledSourceAsymptoticallyBohrAlmostPeriodic`.

Both are Lean-proved equivalent to RH.

**Proved chain.**

```text
literal published first-clock source
→ exact prime creases and von Mangoldt atom recovery
→ rational divisor moments / complete source-divisor tomography
→ Landau exclusion of a right-half divisor
→ RH.
```

The Bohr form adds:

```text
continuous asymptotic Bohr model
→ bounded source tail
→ eventual lower floor
→ RH.
```

HTGeometry proves summable-spectrum screw almost periodicity, exact Bohr coefficients,
uniqueness, recurrence, full tail closure, and Torelli recovery once an asymptotic model
exists. For finite rationally independent phase packets it also proves a separated-tolerance
Haar recurrence-frequency sandwich. That finite theorem supplies neither the literal CCM
identification nor dimension-uniform control for an infinite prime packet. RHTower proves
complete single-clock source/divisor tomography.

The Bohr algebra now includes an exact product-spectrum law: for bounded continuous
almost-periodic functions represented by bounded continuous maps, the product frequency module
lies in the join of the two factor modules; a parallel theorem passes the same containment from
uniform trigonometric approximation. This closes a generic frequency-bookkeeping step, not the
literal first-clock asymptotic model or its uniform prime-tail approximation.

Finite aperture phase geometry is now source-concrete. The prime Dirichlet polynomial has exactly
the rational frequency module spanned by `log p` for `p ≤ N`, of rank `π(N)`. For every `σ > 0`,
the cutoff Euler product is a uniform limit of positive-coefficient exponent-vector polynomials,
has the same exact frequency module, and its Bohr hull character packet surjects onto the full
prime torus `T^{π(N)}`. Thus finite-aperture phase dimension and torus coverage are proved for the
literal Euler observable; neither statement controls the infinite-prime tail nor orients the
first-clock scalar.

Three exact guardrails make the recurrence premise irreducible to weaker analytic slogans. A
single Euler factor is Bohr almost periodic with infinite spectrum but rational frequency rank at
most one and satisfies an autonomous first-order algebraic differential equation. Conversely,
`1/(s+2)` is bounded and holomorphic on right-half strips but not almost periodic on any vertical
line, and `sin(s exp s)` is differentially algebraic yet unbounded and non-almost-periodic on every
vertical line. Bounded holomorphy, compact-open normality, differential algebraicity, and finite
frequency rank therefore do not produce the required source recurrence.

The final positive-transport audit closes the last advertised source/response predecessor.
`ccmFirstClockPrimePowerPacket_phaseNatural_sourceFaithful_iff_eq_one` proves that a fixed
source-faithful mixer natural for every physical first-clock phase is the identity, even with
signed entries. Every nonidentity mixer creates an explicit phase-connection defect.
`ccm_orientedSourceFaithfulMarkovTransport_iff_dotProduct_nonnegative` then classifies the most
permissive aperture-dependent positive case: a column-stochastic source-fixing matrix orients all
dual phase coordinates if and only if the original source--phase scalar is nonnegative. Its
rank-one barycentric witness is explicit. On the completed literal CCM packet,
`ccmFirstClockAllCompletedMarkovOrientations_iff_rh` makes universal feasibility exactly RH.

**Terminal target, not an independent open premise.** Either of the following remains a valid
statement of RH:

```text
∃ C ≥ 0, A ≥ π/2, ∀ L ≥ A, -C ≤ B₁(L),
```

or a continuous Bohr-almost-periodic asymptote for the scaled source. Existing completeness
theorems already prove these are RH coordinates; restating them supplies no source acquisition.

**Terminal obstruction.** A zero-frequency top layer, accumulating spectrum, or failure of uniform
remainder separation can evade the existing AP-vanishing theorem. Torelli recovery occurs
after model existence and therefore cannot supply it. Fixed natural transport is identity;
arbitrary positive Markov transport is equivalent to target sign. Remaining signed
connection-current evolution would need a new source-first orientation theorem and is a distinct
route, not an unfinished Route-A connector.

**Assessment.** Clean exact RH endpoint, but no longer counted as an independent proof route. All
advertised positive-factorization predecessors are formally falsified, identity-rigid, or exactly
target-equivalent. Strongest survivor is explicit phase-connection defect plus canonical
barycentric certificate after source sign is known.

### CCM-FU — four-unit source-codiagonal incidence `[OPEN — UNLETTERED LIVE LANE]`

This lane is not Route A. It does not orient the published scalar first clock by a positive

### Route E — critical Möbius/Mertens cancellation [OPEN — SOURCE MISSING]

**Current status.** Two finite/local Route E submechanisms are now classified. A finite prime
aperture containing every prime through additive resolution `N` reproduces the exact Mertens
self-convolution at `N`; its canonical cofinal `N^(2+o(1))` upper law is therefore identical to

The active squarefree Gibbs attack now puts the missing source in a literal all-prime boundary
form.  For `A_z(x)`, the signed squarefree exponential partition using primes at most `z`, unique
factorization gives

```text
Phi(x) = A_z(x) - sum_(p>z) A_z(px) + R_(>=2,z)(x).
```

Choosing `(floor(z^2)+1)x >= (1/2)log(1/x)` bounds the two-large-prime remainder absolutely by
`O(x^(-1/2))`.  Route E is therefore reduced to proving, for every `epsilon>0`,

```text
|A_z(x) - sum_(p>z) A_z(px)| <= C_epsilon x^(-1/2-epsilon).
```

This comparison retains every small-prime parity interaction and the entire moving prime tail; it
is not a finite-aperture surrogate.

The unsigned analytic tail is now closed at its natural moving abscissa. Lean proves

```text
∑_(y<p≤2y) 1/p ≤ (log 2+2C)/log y,
sup_Y ∑_(z<p≤Y) p^(-(1+1/log z)) ≤ 2(log 2+2C)/log 2,
```

with explicit `C=2*mertensFirstConst+1/2`, by sharp Mertens-window control and dyadic summation.
This removes an absolute prime-tail bookkeeping gap in the renewal/Gamma comparison. It cannot
orient Möbius signs, control the zero/near-zero frequency, or produce the co-moving strict-half
flux. Formal anchors: `Sieve/PrimeWindowDyadic.lean` and `Sieve/PrimeTailBound.lean`.

The comparison now has an exact prime-renewal factorization.  With

```text
K_z(t)=exp(-t)-sum_(p>z) exp(-pt)

R_z(t)=sum_(p>z) exp(-(p-1)t),
```

absolute convergence gives

```text
A_z(x)-sum_(p>z)A_z(px)
= sum_(m: every p|m satisfies p<=z) mu(m) K_z(mx).
```

The finite identity is Lean-proved.  On paper, `R_z` is continuous and strictly decreasing from
infinity to zero, so `K_z` has exactly one sign transition.  PNT scaling places it at

Ivić--Tenenbaum squarefree-smooth asymptotics give `Omega_alpha(N)` squarefree

graph and has Gibbs weight bounded below by `exp(-1)`.  Thus every such pruning has
`Omega_alpha(N)` isolated capacity, far above the strict-half target.  In particular, the
proposed `z^(1+o(1))=N^(1/2+o(1))` support cannot work.  The exact gap theorem is
paper- and Lean-proved on the Route E card; the density input is a published friable-number
asymptotic, not a Möbius cancellation assumption.

A separate friable-sector repair is also impossible.  A primary-source re-audit of
de la Bretèche--Tenenbaum's friable Selberg--Delange theorem corrects the specialization to
`f=mu`: for `1<u<2`, `Y=X^(1/u)`, their leading profile is
`psi_(-1)=phi_(-1)''`, not `phi_(-1)`, and

```text
sum_(n<=X, P+(n)<=Y) mu(n)
= -X/log^2 X + O_u(X/log^3 X).
```

Indeed `phi_(-1)(u)=1+log u` and `psi_(-1)(u)=-1/u^2`; the relation
`log Y=log X/u` cancels the aperture dependence exactly.  The normalized profile jumps from
`-1` to `+1` at `u=2`.  The power-smooth signed sector therefore has universal negative
charge `-X/log^2 X`, not strict-half size.  Any successful source must cancel it against the
positive one-large-prime charge across a macroscopic seam.  Critical Route E has
`log N/log z=2-o(1)` and approaches that boundary from the left.  Existing
PNT/zero-free-region estimates control the cancellation only far above square-root scale; its
localized strict-half remainder is still `OPEN-SOURCE-001`.

The corresponding largest-prime renewal has a sharp exponent barrier.  For fixed `1<u<2`,
`Y=X^(1/u)`, its positive operator on an `X^alpha` weighted sup norm has exact norm
`sum_(Y<p<=X)p^(-alpha)`.  PNT gives `log u+o(1)` at `alpha=1`, but
`X^(1-alpha)/((1-alpha)log X)` for every `alpha<1`.  Hence Daboussi's linear-norm
contraction can prove PNT, while scalar renewal induction cannot bootstrap any fixed power
saving.  A surviving source needs signed orthogonality across the prime-scale samples
`M(X/p)` or an equivalent non-scalar structure; stronger absolute estimates are ruled out.

The first exact bridge to the supplied Gamma/co-Poisson source is now proved.  Complete the
large-prime tail by adjoining prime powers with divided-log weight:

```text
B_z^Gamma(x)
 = A_z(x)-sum_(p>z)sum_(k>=1)(1/k)A_z(p^k x).
```

Since `|A_z(y)|<=1/y`,

```text
|B_z^Gamma(x)-(A_z(x)-sum_(p>z)A_z(px))|
 <= x^(-1) sum_(p>z)sum_(k>=2)1/(k p^k)
 <= 1/(xz)=O(x^(-1/2))
```

on the critical aperture.  Thus the primitive completion is equivalent to the literal Route E
boundary at strict-half scale, not a smoothing or stronger assumption.  Its coefficient
`Lambda(p^k)/log(p^k)=1/k` is exactly the Gamma/co-Poisson confluence current divided by one
additional logarithmic action.  `MobiusGammaCoPoissonPrimePowerBoundary.lean` proves the finite
prime/exponent identity and its pre-scalar small-prime parity lift unconditionally.  Remaining
source is now a more sharply typed singular-provider problem.  The divided-log half-density atom
at exponent depth `e+1` differs from the conditional Gamma atom by the exact gauge

```text
p^(e/2)/((e+1)(1-p^(-1))).
```

At depth zero this lies in `[1,2]`; all positive depths form exactly the higher-prime-power tail
already bounded by `O(x^(-1/2))`.  Thus the supplied paper's beyond-finite-Rees depth singularity
cannot create the missing Route E estimate: it acts only on a sector already inside the allowed
error.  The active hard source is a cofinally continuous signed orientation across infinite Wold
breadth on the depth-zero prime face, before its unit codiagonal is scalarized.
`MobiusGammaCoPoissonDepthZeroReduction.lean` proves this axis separation unconditionally.  The
publication-ready superselection paper supplies finite source decomposition and the
Gamma/co-Poisson comparison, but explicitly does not supply the remaining breadth orientation.

The updated superselection theorem now removes one singular scalar escape from Route E as well.
The canonical depth-zero profile is `d(p,0)=p^(-1/2)` on every prime ray and zero at positive
depth, so it is nonzero modulo finite-prime Euler profiles.  A nonzero-frequency phase-node
prime-power profile has active prime-base support at most one and is quotient-zero even when that

Paper proof:
`../2-fully-proven/new_scientific_knowledge/route-e-depth-zero-prime-clock-phase-node-no-go.md`.

A new Beurling firewall now rules out treating that breadth orientation as a consequence of local
prime geometry.  Diamond--Montgomery--Vorhauer's discrete generalized-prime system retains

The firewall is sharp at the generalized-integer counting threshold.  Zhang's normalized
Beurling construction can agree with every ordinary rational prime below any prescribed finite
cutoff and still satisfy

```text
N_P(X)=X+X^(1/2) exp(O((log X)^(2/3)))
```

with analytic continuation through `Re(s)>1/2`, while possessing infinitely many zeros on

mechanism and the `O(x^(-1/2))` higher-large-prime remainder survive.  Thus finite prime agreement,

Any surviving source must detect exact infinite additive self-duality rather than an arbitrarily
accurate finite or asymptotic approximation.
Paper proof: `../2-fully-proven/route-e-beurling-local-axiom-firewall.md`.

That requirement now has a first exact ordinary-lattice realization.  For fixed aperture \(z\),
integer Lambert dilation of the depth-zero boundary satisfies

```text
sum_(k>=1) B_z^(0)(kx)
 = e^(-x)
   - sum_(P^-(n)>z, omega(n)>=2) (omega(n)-1)e^(-nx).
```

Small-prime divisor fibres cancel by Boolean inclusion--exclusion, every rough prime-power fibre
cancels exactly, and the remaining rough-composite tail is one-signed.  At critical aperture it is
`O(x^(-1/2) log(1/x))`, hence already within every strict-half allowance.  Thus forward

`x^(-1/2)/(2(log z)^3)` and is carried by rough squarefree semiprimes; higher breadth and repeated
valuation are exponentially negligible.  But this capacity belongs to the wrong sector:
linearity and the exact zero/one/two-large decomposition give

```text
Lambert(two-or-more-large-prime remainder)
  = positive rough-composite tail.
```

Thus lifting that cone only reconstructs the already-controlled remainder.  The remaining vacuum
inverse is `Lambert^(-1)(e^(-x))=Phi`, the original reciprocal-zeta/RH problem.  This retires
rough-semiprime Lambert return as a source and proves that a survivor must act before Lambert
scalarization or add an independent co-Poisson boundary condition.  Zhang's arbitrary
finite-prefix matching strengthens this to a nonrobustness theorem: no sound strict-half
certificate holding for ordinary primes can be open in finite-prefix topology.  Valid admission
must be nonuniform in the joint aperture/critical-scale limit.

A pre-Lambert second-order producer now survives the first hostile audit.  For two independent
factor replacements, the literal Möbius checkerboard packet satisfies the sharp global law

```text
|exp(-x a0 b0)-exp(-x a1 b0)-exp(-x a0 b1)+exp(-x a1 b1)|

```

Pairwise transport pays either spectator-amplified edge length; the rectangle cancels both

cost `N^(-1/2+o(1))` per packet at `N=1/x`, allowing an `O(N)` fractional cover inside the

construction, doubled when opposite parity requires an `E4`, supplies precisely such
square-root-scale token replacements and keeps their prime factors below the critical aperture.
`MobiusMultiplicativeRectangleCubature.lean` proves the exponential inequality, actual Möbius
corner signs, finite nonnegative weighted packet aggregation, literal physical vertex load,

This is not yet the source theorem, and a second hostile audit corrected its geometry.
Large-prime breadth is additive on coprime products.  Hence every admitted rectangle wholly inside
the zero/one boundary has zero signed coefficient mass in the zero sector and, separately, zero
signed coefficient mass in the one-large sector.  Lean proves both sector-marginal conservation
laws.  A static sector-closed rectangle cover therefore cannot “carry friable bias across the
seam”; it would have to prove each sector small separately, which is false away from integer

The surviving target is `CO-MOVING-RECTANGLE-SEAM-FLUX`: rectangles live on an extended physical
window, their sectorwise opposite-sign pairs cross the moving cutoff, and the induced cutoff
fluxes realize the normalized friable charge `-1` and one-large-prime charge `+1` before their
sum receives the mixed bound.  Pointwise almost-prime supply does not construct that flux or
control corner co-degree.

The analytic mechanism extends through full Wold breadth.  The \(r\)-fold mixed derivative of
\(\exp(-x\prod_i t_i)\) is \(xe^{-y}Q_r(y)\), where \(Q_r\) is the signed Touchard quotient
\(\sum_{k=1}^r(-1)^kS(r,k)y^{k-1}\), and its global norm is at most \(xr^r\).
Thus an admitted \(2^r\)-corner cube costs

```text
x r^r product_i |t_i,1-t_i,0|.
```

The factorial-looking breadth constant is globally affordable.  The elementary bound

```text
#{n<=N: mu(n)!=0, omega(n)=r}
 <= N/r! (sum_{p<=N} 1/p)^r
```

and `r!<=n` imply
`sum_{n<=N,mu(n)!=0} omega(n)^omega(n)=N^(1+o(1))`.
Factorwise square-root gaps therefore aggregate to `N^(1/2+o(1))`, even with unbounded breadth.
The new `COPRIME-WOLD-BLOCK-CUBE-CAPACITY` hard core is simultaneous excluded-prime block
replacement, incoming-corner co-degree, and co-moving seam flux.  Analytic kernel growth is no

An almost-all/fallback hybrid has now been sharply classified.  If ordinary block centers have
gap exponent \(\eta\), an exceptional set of size \(U^{1-\kappa+o(1)}\) uses fallback exponent
\(\theta\), and balanced products satisfy \(N=U^r\), the normalized cube-cost exponent is
\(\max\{\eta,\theta-\kappa\}\).  Thus strict half requires
\(\kappa\ge\theta-\tfrac12\).  The explicit Matomäki--Radziwiłł Möbius exceptional estimate has
usable \(\kappa<10^{-28}\) at its admissible fixed accuracy, while the
\(0.55+\varepsilon\) all-interval arbitrary-sign fallback requires

The sparse-token issue is now quantitative.  A balanced marked \(E_4\) target
\(\ell p_1p_2p_3\) in the published Type-II ranges contributes
\(O(U/(\ell\log\log U\log^2 U))\) physical integers at scale \(U\).  Summing over every prime

A positive survivor now recovers that logarithm on the exceptional sector.  For

\(\gg_\eta U^{1/2+\eta}/(\log U)^{0.45}\) distinct targets, and the dyadic physical

For the one-sided cofactor relation, Hall has also been reduced exactly from arbitrary subsets to

\frac{U^{1/2+\eta}}{(\log U)^{0.45}}
+\frac{|I|}{(\log U)^2}.

This formerly isolated `MOBIUS-EXCEPTIONAL-CARLESON` bound is no longer an independent frontier.
It is one required specialization of full interval Hall expansion for a fixed physical relation

Global exceptional cardinality can imply this interval criterion only across a sharp phase

A subsequent hostile audit separates exceptional windows from unmatched vertices.  In a short
window \(B_j\), optimal complete-bipartite opposite-sign transport leaves exactly

|d_j|,\qquad d_j=\sum_{n\in B_j}\mu(n),

as proved by the existing complete-bipartite Lean theorems.  Fixed-accuracy almost-all input gives

The ordinary source is now compressed exactly rather than heuristically.  Replacing each Möbius
atom in a block of width \(h\) by the block's left representative changes the dyadic Gibbs sum

\(\sum_jd_je^{-x\xi_j}\).  Thus square-root-width blocks preserve the critical exponent.  Discrete
Abel summation and Cauchy--Schwarz then reduce this current to cumulative defects
\(D_j=\sum_{i\le j}d_i\).  On a square-root mesh the exact surviving source law is
\[
\sum_{\xi_j\in[U,2U]}M(\xi_j)^2\le U^{3/2+o(1)},
\]
or, for general width \(h\le U^{1/2+o(1)}\),
\(\sum M(\xi_j)^2\le U^{2+o(1)}/h\). This energy is sufficient for critical Gibbs decay, but the
earlier \(U^{3/4}\) sampled-spike interpretation was false for Mertens: bounded increments force

turns the mesh law into the classical all-\(\varepsilon\) full Mertens mean-square scale
\(\sum_{U<n\le2U}M(n)^2\le U^{2+o(1)}\). It is therefore an RH-bearing bridge, not a new
arithmetic producer. `MobiusBlockDefectCompression.lean` and
`MobiusBlockDefectEnergy.lean` formalize the finite reduction unconditionally.  Published
almost-all results control increments \(d_j\), not this accumulated low-frequency energy; the
two-phase countermodel has small increments and zero total but violates the energy law.
`MOBIUS-CRITICAL-MESH-ENERGY` is an exact RH-bearing Mertens mean-square restatement, not a source
producer. The live hard core is construction of `R_X` and its physical expansion/flux theorem;

Co-moving flux now shares the same exact analytic coordinate.  Static rectangle

\quad(k=0,1)

Menon's mean square permits ordinary defect mass at the latter scale, and the exact Lean

The obvious synthesis of Menon's local mean square with the \(0.55\) all-interval theorem is also

One pointwise supply gate is now closed on paper.  Extracting the factor ranges and count from the

On the analytic side, failure of RH activates a maximal open escape cone: finite Möbius
Gamma–Hankel off-diagonal covariance tends to `+infinity` on every line
`1/2 < sigma < 1/2 + xiRightTransverseSup`. Conversely, frequently bounded returns at one line
force `xiRightTransverseSup <= sigma-1/2`. Thus covariance detects the right-zero abscissa; it is
not smoothed into boundedness by finite-prime completion.

**Current Wold-breadth, Hall-capacity, and sieve boundary.** Four unconditional results sharply
separate local supply from the missing global cancellation:

- every interval with `h / log log x → ∞` contains both Möbius product signs in balanced
  coprime factor pairs;
- reciprocal prime markers and the Menon identity give at least `C H / log X` physical
  prime-anchor targets of each sign;
- growing markers transport every fixed lower or upper deviation from normal Wold breadth with
  an explicit exponential tail rate;
- every fixed finite polynomial moment family in `ω(n)` is identical on the two Möbius
  parities.

The first theorem closes Boolean collision normalization but leaves the physical Hall map and
co-moving flux. The second closes the prime-anchor Hall gate. The third removes every fixed
off-normal breadth tail; the survivor is the exact normal-order seam. The fourth proves that no
fixed finite collection of Wold-breadth moments can orient Möbius parity. The formalized patch-seam
theorem further proves that internal polynomial seams telescope as an exact coboundary. Growing
overlapping patches remain possible; their remaining issue is physical ordinary-integer
adjacency, not scalar seam gluing.

The formal `RHTower/Sieve` layer supplies interval-multiple counts, truncated Möbius and
Bonferroni inequalities, Brun/Selberg coprime upper bounds, explicit Mertens first and second
prime-sum estimates, smooth/rough decomposition, Hardy--Ramanujan recursion and Chernoff/Shiu
`ω`-tails, and Farey/Parseval/large-sieve infrastructure. The sharp proper-Farey inequality has
constant `Q²+4πN` and yields Montgomery's sieve and the corresponding omitted-class bounds.
The Dirichlet-polynomial branch proves mean-value, Gallagher reduction, derivative-energy,
large-values, and large-values-count estimates; its absolute row-sum proof retains the explicit
logarithmic loss and does not claim the sharp Montgomery--Vaughan form. These are unconditional
counting and energy inputs. The finite localization bridge is also exact: an arbitrary continuous
kernel pairs termwise with a finite Dirichlet polynomial, the symmetric phase integral is
`2 sin(TL)/L` away from `L=0` and `2T` at zero, and its norm is bounded by both `2T` and
`2/|L|`. Thus the corpus can isolate finite logarithmic-frequency packets without a convergence
assumption. This bridge remains unsigned and finite; it does **not** define the physical relation
`R_X`, prove full interval Hall expansion for it, or construct the co-moving central-breadth flux.

The newest committed fibre layer also identifies exactly what the published
Matomäki--Radziwiłł bilinear input would have to supply. `PairFibre.lean` rewrites the balanced
product sum as an outer dyadic block of moving short-interval fibres;
`HarmonicDyadicBlock.lean` supplies the limiting harmonic mass `log 2`; and
`MRFibreReduction.lean` proves that the Möbius bilinear statement follows from one named averaged

coprime-product-sign subproblem, not the Route-E producer: it neither fixes an ordinary-integer
relation `R_X` nor proves its central Hall expansion or cutoff-crossing flux.

**Endpoints.**

- critical or strict-half Mertens bounds;
- half-polylog discrete Laplace decay;
- additive triangle and second-Cesàro bounds;
- `MobiusLaplaceCofinalGammaHankelOffDiagonalFrequentlyUpperBounded`.

**Proved chain.**

```text
critical Möbius decay or cofinally bounded off-diagonal covariance
→ Mellin continuation / strict-right L² energy
→ ζ nonvanishing in Re(s) > 1/2
→ RH.
```

The Gamma–Hankel split removes diagonal and lower-bound obligations. Only sparse upper
bounded returns are needed.

Two source-classification laws are now unconditional Lean theorems:

```text
finite prime aperture covers all primes <= N
→ exact coefficient recovery through N
→ exact Mertens partial sums on every cutoff <= N
→ exact Mertens self-convolution at N;

canonical finite-aperture N^(2+o(1)) law
↔ second-Cesàro Möbius source law
→ RH;

not RH
→ covariance escape on every
  1/2 < sigma < 1/2 + xiRightTransverseSup;

frequently bounded returns at sigma
→ xiRightTransverseSup <= sigma-1/2.

Gibbs zero/one-large-prime boundary comparison
→ every strict-half Möbius--Laplace bound
→ RH;

Möbius-specific additive-circle subpower evaluation
→ every strict-half Möbius--Laplace bound
→ RH.
```

Finite additive-circle Parseval, the flat-polynomial hostile model, two-prime Rayleigh negativity,
the cardinality coalescence/tangent identities, and the exact divisor--Möbius dilation
diagonalization are now unconditional Lean infrastructure.  The latter shows that the complete

missing cancellation.  Lean also proves Hall selection from an explicit neighbor family,
exponential displacement consumption, symmetric parity-flow cancellation, and the canonical
minimum-endpoint conductance defect bound; none assumes the arithmetic incidence estimate.

The Plücker normal-cone branch now has a cofinal diagonal on `Re(s) > 1`: for every
positive critical sequence tending to zero, Lean constructs a monotone cofinal exhaustion
by finite prime sets whose Euler-normal source transfers converge locally uniformly to the
reciprocal squarefree section. The same diagonal survives multiplication by the inverse
archimedean factor and projectivization to the negative reciprocal-square horizon. Fixed

finite-prime exhaustion with the Euler-boundary and Gamma-completion limits, but all

Verjovsky's shrinking-arc local-moment theorem gives another exact Mertens coordinate:
subpolynomial local moments for every finite order, or for an unbounded admissible set of
orders, are equivalent to RH. Its unconditional moment-to-point inequality converts local
`L^q` control into a bound for `M(N)`, but supplies no Möbius local-moment estimate. It is
therefore an upstream Route E connector, not a new source producer, and is not formalized.

**Open core.** First define, independently of every observed Möbius partial-sum imbalance, one
explicit source-predeclared physical relation `R_X` on squarefree integers in the central normal
Wold sector `omega(n)=(1+o(1)) log log X`. Then prove at `H=X^(1/2+o(1))`, simultaneously:

1. normalized row load and physical incoming co-degree at most one;
2. interval Hall expansion for every subinterval using distinct opposite-parity integers, not
   incidence labels;
3. crossing of the moving zero/one-large-prime cutoff with friable charge `-1` transported against
   one-large-prime charge `+1`; and
4. total mixed-difference Gibbs cost `X^(1/2+o(1))`.

This is `SOURCE-POINTED-BIFILTERED-CENTRAL-HALL`. Its current card is not fully frozen because
`R_X` has no formula: “construct from factorization/additive/co-Poisson data” is only an
admissibility condition. Existential Hall transport risks collapsing to complete-bipartite flow,

axis; positive valuation depth is already inside the permitted tail. Existing sieve counts,

**Killed mechanisms.**

- fixed-order Gowers uniformity supplies only logarithmic savings;
- second fixed-prime removal has a negative lobe;
- ternary divisor insertion pays excessive mass;
- Lambert lifting collapses to the binary source or loses two powers;
- positive semiprime LCM blocks have excessive cubic mass.

- full dilation Gram diagonalization reduces every all-scale quadratic estimate to the same signed

**Positive survivor.** Gamma–Hankel covariance is a quantitative zero-abscissa detector: one

### Route F — all-prime Sonin transfer [RETIRED]

**Terminal status.** Current Route F mechanism is closed negatively. For every geometric Burnol
log-shell conductor and every arbitrary schedule of finite tower heights, its literal quadratic
pairing on the canonical source packets has only affine-logarithmic order and fails the typed lower

**Unconditional progress.**

- aperture refinement is strictly subadditive;
- lost-layer coupling energy is positive and divergent;
- inverse-dilated packet physical cutoff and physical-band error vanish exactly;

- the remaining coupling-band budget is exactly three packet-frequency norms at radii
  `rb`, `ra`, and `b`;

- the packet mean is eliminated from that bound, giving one annulus-free expression
  uniform over every packet in a shell cut;
- a sharper replacement retains the mean's own tail estimate rather than bounding it by

- division by the explicit norm floor has a closed form: each sharp band slack is the
  gap-tail factor times the square root of band radius divided by shell width and fixed

- the reference tail majorant absorbs every fixed polynomial weight, including the square-

- the packet norm floor is explicit and positive, and the resulting energy theorem has no

- the shell schedule itself is now explicit: tying the scale to a cut into `N` annuli makes

- one product can be chosen to satisfy both packet-tail conditions and both product-driven

- right composition by the moving dilation preserves Hilbert–Schmidt energy, so the

- one fixed schedule product and starting cut now give a single admissible sequence of

- one analytic comparison also excludes every logarithmically capped additive term in the

- more generally, every sublinear scalar additive term `f(scale)` in the typed

- every uniformly bounded family of continuous linear operators paired quadratically against

- more generally, even an unbounded channel family fails when its images on the bounded packet

- the canonical unscaled Sonin packet has a uniform twelfth-order pointwise spectral-density tail

- for the actual frequency-side logarithmic channel, the exact shell-energy split separates

**Terminal verdict.** Bounded, sublinear, logarithmic, fixed-height, arbitrary-height, position,

### Route G — Suzuki Hermite–Biehler, anti-Pick, and Ward realization `[OPEN / NARROWED]`

**Endpoint.** `SuzukiHermiteBiehler xiHedenmalmDeBrangesE` implies RH without a
simplicity hypothesis.  This remains a valid consumer theorem.  It is not Suzuki's published
producer: Corollary 1.6 proposes one holomorphically gauged entire characteristic converging
compact-open on all of `C` to `z^2 XiC/XiC'`.

**Proved finite/source chain.**

```text
finite self-adjoint extensions and interlacing
→ finite anti-Pick identity
→ signed partial fractions and residue signs
→ measure-level anti-Pick law
→ half-plane Herglotz representation and support theorem.
```

The self-adjoint-extension interlacing in this chain is not parity interlacing. Literal parity
reduction gives `Q_even=T_a+H_a` and `Q_odd=T_a-H_a`. For every aperture, a corner
`(0,b)^2` with `2b<log 2` contains no prime seam, and there

```text
H_a(x,y)
 =exp((x+y)/2)
  -exp(-5(x+y)/2)/(1-exp(-2(x+y))).
```

Distinct translates of this archimedean profile are linearly independent on every nonempty
positive interval: clearing translate denominators gives an entire relation, and evaluation at
the distinct poles `z=-y_j` kills every coefficient. Hence this corner, `H_a`, and
`Q_even-Q_odd=2H_a` all have infinite rank. The two parity blocks are never rank-one or
finite-rank perturbations. Donoghue interlacing for two boundary angles therefore cannot be
imported as a source theorem for `T_a+H_a` versus `T_a-H_a`. Accidental or genuinely
infinite-rank parity interlacing remains possible. Paper proof and unconditional analytic Lean
are in `suzuki-parity-hankel-infinite-rank-no-go.md` and
`SuzukiArchimedeanParityHankelInfiniteRank.lean`.

The same prime-free corner now has a sharp indefinite classification. For `x,y>0`,

```text
H_a(x,y)
 =p(x)p(y)-sum_(k>=0) q_k(x)q_k(y),

p(x)=exp(x/2),
q_k(x)=exp(-(5/2+2k)x).
```

For any `m+1` distinct positive nodes, the first `m+1` negative moments factor through the
Vandermonde matrix on `r_j=exp(-2x_j)`. On the kernel of the single positive moment `P`, a
subspace of dimension at least `m`, every nonzero Gram vector is strictly negative. Conversely,
the positive moment must be injective on every strictly positive subspace, so such a subspace has
dimension at most one. Localization by disjoint bumps transfers the finite Gram witnesses to the
integral form. Hence every aperture has

```text
ind_-(H_a)=ind_-(Q_even-Q_odd)=infinity,
finite-Gram positive index <= 1.
```

Finite-negative-square and finite-index Pontryagin transfers of boundary-angle interlacing are
therefore dead as well. This remains a mechanism no-go, not a Suzuki no-go: comparison with
genuinely infinite negative index, direct credit/debt transport, and the two-angle meromorphic
producer remain live. Paper proof and unconditional finite-Gram Lean are in
`suzuki-parity-hankel-infinite-negative-index.md` and
`SuzukiArchimedeanParityHankelInfiniteNegativeIndex.lean`.

The tempting positive-index-one survivor is itself killed by the first arithmetic seam. The full
positive-ray kernel contains

```text
-Lambda(n)n^(-1/2) delta_(x+y-log n).
```

Whenever `log n<2a`, this is a weighted reflection across the line `x+y=log n`. On reflected bump
packets its swap-even and swap-odd sectors have opposite signs. By taking arbitrarily many
disjoint narrow bump pairs, the bounded regular kernel becomes smaller than the fixed seam weight
while all other prime seams are avoided. Therefore, for every `a>log(2)/2`,

```text
ind_+(H_a)=ind_-(H_a)=infinity,
ind_+(Q_even-Q_odd)=ind_-(Q_even-Q_odd)=infinity.
```

Thus neither orientation of a one-sided finite-index/Pontryagin min--max comparison survives
cofinally. Generic singular-Hankel reflection theory is classical; the new candidate content is
the exact Suzuki first-prime-seam application. Paper proof and unconditional robust finite-packet
Lean are in `suzuki-prime-seam-parity-hankel-strong-indefiniteness.md` and
`SuzukiPrimeSeamParityHankelStrongIndefiniteness.lean`. This still does not exclude a genuinely
arithmetic infinite-seam oscillation theorem or the two-angle meromorphic producer.

Finite projective spectral algebra is now exact: projective chord is a sum of spectral gaps
times source Plücker minors; in two modes its sign is exactly source-minor orientation under
ordered nodes and positive source-weight product. Marked affine secant has a full finite
spectral double sum plus homogeneous channel.

At the abstract compact self-adjoint resolvent level, discreteness is now derived rather
than assumed: real spectral points outside any neighborhood of zero are finite, hence only
finitely many model levels can lie in a fixed negative band. This removes the auxiliary
finiteness premise from the finite Morse completion once a compact source resolvent and its
level identification are supplied; it does not construct either object.

The generic resolvent-coordinate limit is also closed. If a bounded operator `B` is self-adjoint,
injective, and has real spectrum in `[0,M]`, continuous functional calculus proves
`((s-ρ)x/(1+s x))(B)v → v` strongly for every vector. The proof is uniform only after composing
the error with `B`; the unscaled symbols fail to converge uniformly at spectral accumulation
point zero. This constructs no unbounded `A`, source resolvent `B=(A-ρ)⁻¹`, or arithmetic spectral
identification, so it does not establish Suzuki's conjectural finite-to-global limit.

The critical prime-power wall field now has a positive Hermitian carrier without erasing
phase: every finite height section is an exact rank-one Gram matrix, its diagonal is the
positive von-Mangoldt wall weight, and its functional-equation-reflected anti-diagonal is
the far delayed-hinge numerator. This identifies the right positivity type but does not
orient an aggregate of distinct source atoms; rank one at each atom is not the global
chord-mass theorem.

The wall branch extends further. Finite reflected-current packets have an exact
positive-minus-negative light-cone Gram completion. Cubic aperture dressing gives an
all-prime Hilbert completion; summing valuation depth supplies a sharp positive floor;
and coherent causal depth fusion gives an absolutely convergent all-prime cross-prime
Gram with a faithful height separator. For the affine inverse-Krein defect, the negative
part is also the unique least—and hence the unique Loewner-minimal—continuous functional-
calculus correction among commuting positive completions. The completed aperture carrier
nevertheless has height-flat diagonal and cannot by itself supply Xi curvature, and the
abstract least correction has not been identified with the literal arithmetic source.

**Completion/provider closure.** The compact BV/co-Poisson candidate is now closed by a
dependency-closed paper and Lean no-go. For the canonical sharp source, ordinary Burnol `L2`

**Closed one-angle subclass.** `SuzukiHolomorphicGaugeCharacteristicLimitWall` quantifies over
every eventually entire real-zero characteristic family and every eventually entire exponential

```text
not_suzukiHolomorphicGaugeCharacteristicLimitWall
```

This theorem is independent of aperture, shift, extension angle, and source operator, so it is a

**Closed operator phase-selection subclass.** Primary-source recheck of Suzuki Sections 7.3--7.6

compactly supported smooth functions are dense and invariant under strongly continuous unitary

```text
closure(D_infinity)=D_(pi/2).
```

Consequently finite deficiency projections converge strongly to zero. Every contractive
extension-angle schedule has same generalized strong-resolvent limit; no phase lock selects a

`suzuki-finite-aperture-boundary-phase-locking.md`, whose universal stable-rank theorem remains
