This still does not force locally normalized moving defect functions to converge to zero, so it
does not close scalar two-angle meromorphic producer.

**Closed regular unit-response / finite-Rees post-fusion subclass.** The updated biaxial theorem
in `riemann-source-modular-superselection/main-universal.tex` classifies one precise possible
realization of a Suzuki source proof. Its hypotheses are conjunctive and exact:

```text
same unit response on every positive exponent shell
+ orthogonal ell2 or Bessel shell modes
+ reflected prime-valuation sewing at one finite Rees grade
+ terminal sign formed only after reflection-invariant scalar fusion.
```

Breadth and depth fail independently. Before scalarization, an `N`-shell reciprocal packet splits
into one fused hyperbolic divisor block and a centered transverse block of signature `(N-1,N-1)`.
Countably many unit-response modes make the orthogonal codiagonal nonclosable and exclude a bounded
constant-response Bessel reader even at zero charge. At nonzero reflected charge, every exact
bounded sewing has norm beyond every finite inverse-Wold Rees power. Reflection-invariant terminal
scalarization then kills the odd modular charge. This classification is formalized by
`CommonCharacterFusionConservation.lean`, `HighExponentShellFusionConservation.lean`, and
`PrimeValuationHellingerReesReflectionFracture.lean`.

The source-specific quantitative connector is now also closed. Suzuki's interval `[-a,a]` sees
exactly the arithmetic translations `log n` with `n<=exp(2a)`. At integer cutoff `X`, put
`a_X=(1/2)log(X+1)`. Harmonic-sum/Euler-product domination gives

```text
log(X+1) <= primeWoldBoundaryVolumeBelow(X+1)^(-1).
```

Consequently every exact reflected Hellinger sewing in the theorem's valuation architecture obeys,
for every fixed `K`,

```text
a_X^K = o(||R_(X+1)||),
||R_(X+1)|| is not O(a_X^K).
```

Thus polynomially conditioned reflected sewing is impossible in physical Suzuki aperture, not
merely in an abstract Rees coordinate. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-aperture-rees-superpolynomial-conditioning.md` and
`SuzukiApertureReesConditioningNoGo.lean`.

**Shifted deficiency and Pick-transfer boundaries.** Real zeros of Suzuki's shifted
finite-aperture characteristic functions do not orient the unshifted Weil form. Variational Lean
proves that shift admissibility is exactly the inequality placing the shift below the aperture
floor (with attainment for the converse), and that a cofinal admissible shift line tending to zero
exists exactly when no nested floor is negative. Through the localized restricted-Weil criterion,
zero shift at every aperture or such a cofinal line is already RH-strength. The surviving operator
problem is therefore an indefinite unshifted determinant-line construction, not a choice of safer
negative shifts.

At paper level, every even real-rooted finite spectral factor has a positive logarithmic-derivative
Pick kernel, and convergence of its dephased logarithmic derivatives to the centered Xi logarithmic
derivative on the Euler half-plane would prove RH. The canonical CCM aperture phase subtracts
`2 log(lambda)` times the positive half-plane Cauchy kernel. Thus finite real spectra alone do not
transport Pick positivity. A valid producer must either control the relative spectral discrepancy
after removal of this Weyl background or construct a different pole-compatible two-angle limit.
This is a sharpened form of the existing convergence/open-orientation core, not a second Route G.

**Unconditional Xi first-defect cone and exact source obligation.** Normalize the Xi Donoghue
target at `i`, take its Livšic transform `Theta_Xi`, and put

```text
kappa_Xi = Re(2i Theta_Xi'(i)).
```

Multiplicity-exact squared-zero moments, the unconditional zero-height gap
`Im(rho)^2>41/2`, and the open critical strip give the atomwise inequality

```text
85 Re(r_q^2) < 4 Re(r_q).
```

After summation and differentiation of the Xi logarithmic derivative this proves, without RH,

```text
77/85 < kappa_Xi < 1,
2i Theta_Xi'(i) = kappa_Xi in R.
```

Consequently an Xi-identifying Suzuki source has normalized parity shares

```text
even share > 81/85,
0 < odd share < 4/85.
```

Locally uniform convergence of the actual disk-conjugated finite Suzuki Livšic carriers forces
their exact source defect angles to tend to `kappa_Xi`; higher-order-only noncollapse cannot
identify Xi. Combining this convergence with the ground-pole law `1-kappa <= 2 rho_ground`
forces the fixed positive eventual leakage floor

```text
(1-kappa_Xi)/4 < rho_ground.
```

Thus both free collapse (`kappa→0`) and ground saturation/Cayley universality (`kappa→1`,
`rho_ground→0`) are quantitatively excluded. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-xi-first-defect-jet-gap.md`, `SuzukiXiFirstDefectJet.lean`, and
`SuzukiXiFirstDefectJetSource.lean`.

**Mixed-ground residue selector.** The earlier canonical shift selector used the sufficient
endpoint hypothesis of a simple pure-even spectral floor, forcing `kappa(lambda)→1`. That
hypothesis is not necessary. If the pole-rescaled even and odd source energies satisfy

```text
(lambda_a-lambda)E(lambda)→m_E,
(lambda_a-lambda)O(lambda)→m_O,
```

with `m_E+m_O>0`, then the ground endpoint is exactly

```text
kappa_g=(m_E-m_O)/(m_E+m_O).                              (G.0)
```

Hence the same source-defined greatest matching shift exists whenever the free endpoint is below
`kappa_Xi` and

```text
(1+kappa_Xi)m_O < (1-kappa_Xi)m_E.                        (G.0a)
```

For `m_E>0,m_O>=0`, this is the sharp source-polarization threshold

```text
m_O/m_E < tau_Xi,
tau_Xi=(1-kappa_Xi)/(1+kappa_Xi),
0<tau_Xi<4/81.                                           (G.0b)
```

Arbitrary bottom multiplicity and mixed parity are now admitted; no monotonicity of the defect
path or uniqueness of a matching root is needed. The source selector is its greatest root. The
hard cofinal first-jet obligation sharpens further in the literal bottom projection. Let
`P_(0,a)` be the bottom spectral projection, `Jf(x)=f(-x)`, and
`w_a=P_(0,a)e^x`. Reflection invariance gives `Jw_a=P_(0,a)e^(-x)` and equal norms. Then (G.0a)
is exactly

```text
kappa_Xi ||w_a||^2 < <Jw_a,w_a>,                         (G.0c)

2||P_(0,a)sinh(x)||^2 < (1-kappa_Xi)||w_a||^2,           (G.0d)

||w_a-Jw_a||^2 < 2(1-kappa_Xi)||w_a||^2.                 (G.0e)
```

The unconditional `77/85<kappa_Xi` forces

```text
<Jw_a,w_a> > (77/85)||w_a||^2,
||P_(0,a)sinh(x)||^2/||w_a||^2 < 4/85,
||w_a-Jw_a||^2 < (16/85)||w_a||^2,
||P_(0,a)cosh(x)||^2 > (81/4)||P_(0,a)sinh(x)||^2.       (G.0f)
```

Thus a visible pure-even bottom source is automatically admitted, a visible pure-odd bottom
source is excluded, and a parity-floor collision is controlled by one sharp reflection-fidelity
cone. A source-invisible bottom eigenspace has zero residue and is a separate finite-endpoint
case; spectral bottom existence alone does not supply the selector.

The named live conjecture `SUZUKI-GROUND-REFLECTION-CONE` is that (G.0c) holds for the literal
restricted-Weil projection on one cofinal aperture family. This is a prime--archimedean spectral
statement, not a consequence of abstract positivity, and it is only the pole-driven sufficient
branch.

**Interior-excursion selector.** Failure of (G.0c) does not kill Suzuki. If the ground endpoint
avoids `kappa_Xi` and one finite shift satisfies

```text
lambda_0<lambda_a,
kappa_Xi<kappa_a(lambda_0),                              (G.0g)
```

then the already cofinally smaller free endpoint and continuity give a matching root. Both
non-target endpoint limits compactly trap the entire matching set, so its supremum is again its
greatest member, without monotonicity or root uniqueness. At a finite shift (G.0g) is exactly

```text
(1+kappa_Xi)O_a(lambda_0)
  <(1-kappa_Xi)E_a(lambda_0).                            (G.0h)
```

Thus cofinal first-jet selection has two live, source-literal branches: the bottom-pole reflection
cone (G.0c), or an interior parity-resolvent excursion (G.0h). A negative Suzuki closure must prove
both fail. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-interior-excursion-shift-selection.md` and
`SuzukiInteriorExcursionShiftSelection.lean`.

The interior branch has an exact spectral transport budget. Define

```text
L_a(lambda)=log(E_a(lambda)/O_a(lambda)),
H_a(lambda)=E_a'(lambda)/E_a(lambda)
            -O_a'(lambda)/O_a(lambda),
R_Xi=(1+kappa_Xi)/(1-kappa_Xi)>81/4.                    (G.0i)
```

For the literal parity spectral measures, `E_a'` and `O_a'` are squared-resolvent matrix
elements, so the two normalized terms in `H_a` are inverse-gap means. On every positive finite
shift interval,

```text
integral_(lambda_1)^(lambda_2) H_a(lambda) dlambda
 =L_a(lambda_2)-L_a(lambda_1),                           (G.0j)

kappa_Xi<kappa_a(lambda_2)
iff
integral_(lambda_1)^(lambda_2) H_a(lambda) dlambda
 >log(R_Xi)-L_a(lambda_1).                               (G.0k)
```

At the far-negative free end,

```text
E_free/O_free
 =[sinh(2a)+2a]/[sinh(2a)-2a] -> 1.                     (G.0l)
```

Thus a cofinal interior excursion must accumulate an order-one even-over-odd lower-spectrum
advantage approaching the exact cost `log R_Xi`, and at least the unconditional floor
`log(81/4)`. This is stronger than noncollapse and points directly at the arithmetic
`T_a+H_a` versus `T_a-H_a` spectral measures. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-parity-resolvent-transport-budget.md` and
`SuzukiParityResolventTransportBudget.lean`.

The existing global monotone-likelihood producer cannot sign this budget: with parity response
`+1/-1`, comonotonicity forces every visible odd node below every visible even node, while the
opposite orientation forces the reverse. Bi-unbounded visible parity spectra rule out both
globally. The required theorem is therefore a weighted signed cross-parity transport estimate,
not global spectral separation.

That estimate now has a literal infinite-spectrum carrier. Let `t` and `s` be the even and odd
spectral variables for the source measures `mu_(+,a)` and `mu_(-,a)`. Exact Fubini factorization
gives

```text
E_a'(lambda)O_a(lambda)-E_a(lambda)O_a'(lambda)
 =double_integral
   (s-t)/[(t-lambda)^2(s-lambda)^2]
   dmu_(+,a)(t)dmu_(-,a)(s).                            (G.0m)
```

Write `C_a(lambda)` and `D_a(lambda)` for the positive and negative Jordan masses of this
integrable pair kernel. Then

```text
H_a(lambda)
 =[C_a(lambda)-D_a(lambda)]/[E_a(lambda)O_a(lambda)],

H_a(lambda)>0
iff
D_a(lambda)<C_a(lambda).                                (G.0n)
```

Thus every even node below an odd node supplies lower-pair credit, while every odd-below-even
pair incurs spectral-inversion debt with the exact squared-resolvent weight. Arbitrarily many
local inversions are allowed. The cofinal interior theorem is precisely

```text
integral_(lambda_1)^(lambda_2)
 [C_a(lambda)-D_a(lambda)]/[E_a(lambda)O_a(lambda)] dlambda
 >log(R_Xi)-L_a(lambda_1).                              (G.0o)
```

When both interval endpoints are below the Xi wall and an interior point is above it, the
transport has a forced ordered turning pattern. There are
`lambda_up<lambda_max<lambda_down` in the interval with

```text
D_a(lambda_up)<C_a(lambda_up),
D_a(lambda_max)=C_a(lambda_max),
C_a(lambda_down)<D_a(lambda_down),
kappa_Xi<kappa_a(lambda_max).                           (G.0p)
```

This follows from the exact positive-factor law

```text
kappa_a'
 =2E_a O_a/(E_a+O_a)^2 *
   [E_a'/E_a-O_a'/O_a],                                (G.0q)
```

the extreme-value theorem, Fermat's theorem, and the mean-value theorem. Thus a genuine
two-sub-target-endpoint excursion cannot have one-signed hazard: it must pass from credit
domination through exact credit/debt balance to debt domination. Paper proof and unconditional
Lean are in `SuzukiInteriorExcursionSpectralTurningPoint.lean`.

The same `lambda_max` satisfies a third-resolvent wall. Let `pi_+` and `pi_-` be the probability
measures obtained by tilting the literal parity measures by
`1/(node-lambda_max)` and normalizing by `E_a(lambda_max)` and `O_a(lambda_max)`;
write `X(u)=1/(u-lambda_max)`. Then

```text
E_a(lambda_max)/O_a(lambda_max)>R_Xi>81/4,
E_a'(lambda_max)/E_a(lambda_max)
 =O_a'(lambda_max)/O_a(lambda_max),
Var_pi+(X)<=Var_pi-(X).                                 (G.0r)
```

Indeed, at normalized-mean balance,

```text
kappa_a''(lambda_max)
 =4E_a O_a/(E_a+O_a)^2 *
   [Var_pi+(X)-Var_pi-(X)] <= 0.                        (G.0s)
```

Thus an interior source proof must create a high-ratio stationary parity pair with equal tilted
inverse-gap means and ordered dispersion, not merely positive integrated credit. The
log-transform variance identity is classical; candidate novelty is its forced Suzuki/Xi
incidence at the same literal credit/debt balance point. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are
in `suzuki-parity-resolvent-transport-budget.md` and
`SuzukiInteriorExcursionSpectralTurningPoint.lean`.

This is measure-level, not a finite truncation. The four first/squared-resolvent integrability
hypotheses imply integrability of the pair kernel. The named live source statement
`SUZUKI-PARITY-SPECTRAL-TRANSPORT` is that cofinally either the bottom reflection cone (G.0c)
holds or one literal interval pays (G.0o). It is the exact within-Suzuki-carrier admission
obligation, not its proof and not a new route. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-parity-resolvent-transport-budget.md`,
`SuzukiParityResolventTransportBudget.lean`, and
`SuzukiParitySpectralPairTransport.lean`, with the forced turning law in
`SuzukiInteriorExcursionSpectralTurningPoint.lean`.

The source-horizon law adds a separate provenance gate. Let `E_src(rho)` be the injective
mass-one high-exponent source and let `T_lambda` be any bounded linear source-to-parity family.
If every `T_lambda` is label-blind, its full shift profiles at `rho` and `1-conj(rho)` are equal.
No nonlinear downstream operation on those profiles—including hazard ratios, Jordan
credit/debt, maxima, or (G.0o)—can recover the reflection distinction. Hence any bounded
reflection-separating Suzuki carrier must be label-sensitive at some shift; otherwise the
producer must leave the bounded category through singular, nonclosable, or relation-valued
transport. This does not assert that Suzuki's literal source map factors through the shell frame,
and does not exclude a genuinely nonlinear producer.

The updated complete Boolean-shell theorem sharpens, but does not bypass, this warning. Every
finite differential-field observable has finite shell support and arbitrary shell-field
intersections contain no infinitary ghost. A Suzuki aperture therefore cannot acquire hidden
finite-stage provenance from completion. However locally uniform, weak, and normal-family limits
are not finite differential-field operations; growing finite supports can converge to a
common-core infinite source. Using the no-ghost theorem itself to reject the two-angle cofinal
limit would therefore be invalid. A valid no-go must control the actual limiting topology or
prove literal local Clark overcrowding.

The literal position torque now passes the faithful side of this gate. Its prime coefficient
stream is exactly

```text
F_T(n)=n^(1/2) log(n) Lambda(n)(1-n^(-2))
      =Lambda(n)log(n)[sqrt(n)-n^(-3/2)].               (G.0t)
```

It is supported on positive prime powers and is nonzero at every `p^(e+1)`, for every prime `p`
and every depth `e`. The updated exact prime-clock theorem therefore makes its upper-end carrier
hypertranscendental over every field obtained by adjoining the genuine coordinate, all
finite-prime Euler germs, every finite-alphabet residue--valuation fusion carrier at every depth,
and all their differential jets. Thus the position commutator is irreducibly global source data,
not a local-Euler or post-fusion target fit. This authenticates torque provenance but supplies no
missing parity sign: away from `E=O`, fixed `E,O,alpha-beta` still leaves the Hankel gate
surjective onto `R`.

Call the full conjunction `SUZUKI-SOURCE-POINTED-PARITY-TRANSPORT`: construct the parity profile
from a predeclared arithmetic source, prove reflection separation before scalarization by a
label-sensitive or singular mechanism, and prove the ground cone or (G.0o). A freely inhabited
scalar parity profile, even one satisfying (G.0o), is source-provenance theatre.

Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-ground-residue-polarization-selection.md` and
`SuzukiGroundResiduePolarizationSelection.lean`, with the reflection-cone theorem in
`SuzukiGroundProjectionReflectionCone.lean`.

**Exact second-defect/Hankel gate and torque-only no-go.** Write the finite source parity
energies and exponential tilt moments as `E,O,alpha,beta`. For every actual anchored Schur
carrier, Schwarz--Pick and the exact Suzuki endpoint jets now give

```text
kappa = (E-O)/(E+O),
q = 8(alpha O-beta E)/(E+O)^2,
0 <= q+2(1-kappa^2)
   = 8[O(E+alpha)-E beta]/(E+O)^2.                         (G.1)
```

At the normalized Xi target let

```text
A=Re M_0(1),  B=Re M_1(1),  C=Re M_2(1),
```

where `M_n(1)` is the safe-shift source-only prime--Gamma reciprocal moment. Then the identical
Schur slack is

```text
q_Xi+2(1-kappa_Xi^2)=16(AC-B^2)/A^2,                     (G.2)
```

and `A>0`, so its nonnegativity is equivalent without loss to `AC-B^2>=0`. This is the first
nonlinear necessary condition on an Xi-identifying Suzuki limit. It is not the unshifted central
minor and not the full RH-equivalent Hankel tail: it is the exact `y=1` first minor selected by
Suzuki's Donoghue/Livšic normalization.

This safe-shift minor now has a complete multiplicity-exact response/charge decomposition. Put

```text
lambda_q=xiShiftedReciprocalSquare(1,q),
a_q=Re(lambda_q),
tau_q=Re(lambda_q^2)/a_q,
chi_q=Im(lambda_q)^2 |lambda_q|^2/a_q.
```

The unconditional shifted sector gives `a_q>0`, `tau_q>0`, and complete summability. Exact
pointwise cubic algebra and an infinite Cauchy--Schwarz passage give

```text
AC-B^2 = H_Xi-Q_Xi,                                      (G.2a)

H_Xi=A sum_q a_q tau_q^2-(sum_q a_q tau_q)^2 >= 0,
Q_Xi=A sum_q chi_q >= 0,
Q_Xi=0 iff RH.                                           (G.2b)
```

Hence the first nonlinear Suzuki wall is no longer an opaque target minor:

```text
AC-B^2>=0 iff Q_Xi<=H_Xi.                                (G.2c)
```

The source side of this wall is now closed up to one sharp inequality. Lean proves boundary
integrability of every polynomially weighted unit-tilted theta moment, identifies the Xi jets with
the literal probability law proportional to

```text
exp(x) thetaCuspFullSource(x) dx,
```

and, writing its mean, variance, and third cumulant as `mu`, `v`, and `kappa_3`, proves

```text
8(AC-B^2)=mu*kappa_3+(mu-v)(mu+2v),
AC-B^2>=0
  iff -mu*kappa_3 <= (mu-v)(mu+2v).                       (G.2d)
```

It also proves `mu>0`, `v>0`, `kappa_3<0`, `v<mu`, and strict positive definiteness of every
finite raw-moment Hankel block of this full-support endpoint law. Thus boundary completion,
moment identification, and the sign chamber are no longer open. The sole source inequality at
this subgate is the theta-specific skewness budget in `(G.2d)`. Generic Hankel positivity does not
imply it because the safe minor is a nonlinear combination of cumulants rather than a raw moment
minor. Even proving it would close only this necessary safe wall, not the cofinal two-angle
producer, source selection, or Xi identification. Formal anchor:
`SuzukiSafeBoundaryThetaCumulantWall.lean`.

Every genuinely off-line atom pays strictly positive transverse charge; only cross-orbit shifted
response dispersion can cancel it. This is not a proof of `(G.2c)`: neither sector positivity nor
positivity of all individual low powers controls that competition. It is the sharp target
classification exposed by the live producer. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-safe-shift-response-charge-cancellation.md` and
`SuzukiSafeShiftResponseChargeCancellation.lean`.

The two sides of this competition now have a quantitative separation.  If
`r_q=xiZeroCopyReciprocalSquare(q)`, `mu_0=normalizedPrimeArchMoment(0)`, and
`E=sum_q Im(r_q)^2`, exact inverse-coordinate algebra gives

```text
safe response mass(q) < Re(r_q),
chi_q <= (4/85) Im(r_q)^2,

Q_Xi <= (4/85) mu_0 E
     < 116/7,968,750,000.                                (G.2d)
```

The response slopes also tend to zero off finite sets while remaining strictly positive. Infinite
Xi divisor support therefore makes them nonconstant, and the Hilbert term has the exact centered
form

```text
H_y=Re M_0(y) sum_q a_q(tau_q-bar_tau_y)^2 > 0
for every y>0.                                           (G.2e)
```

Thus the safe wall is no longer a comparison of two merely nonnegative unknowns. Its negative
channel is below an explicit source scale and its positive channel is genuinely nondegenerate.
The remaining quantitative target problem is the single sufficient floor

```text
116/7,968,750,000 <= H_1,                                (G.2f)
```

which would force `AC-B^2>0`. Strict positivity `(G.2e)` does not prove `(G.2f)`, and replacing the
missing floor by that qualitative fact would be invalid. Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in
`suzuki-safe-shift-transverse-charge-budget.md` and
`SuzukiSafeShiftTransverseChargeBudget.lean`.

The literal restricted-Weil position commutator has also been determined. Multiplication by
position produces the logarithmically weighted Mangoldt torque; its completed tent transform is
an exact difference of Xi logarithmic curvatures, and the full source torque changes strict sign
cofinally in both directions. A resolvent expansion promotes this observable to the actual
deficiency anomaly for fixed aperture and sufficiently negative admissible shift.

Moreover, the exact prime numerator is the source `(G.0t)`. Every prime ray is active at every
valuation depth, so exact source--analytic correspondence proves its carrier hypertranscendental
over complete local-Euler residue--valuation fusion. The torque is therefore a faithful global
prime-clock observable. What fails below is not provenance but information content: one scalar
commutator coordinate cannot determine the two independent parity tilt moments.

This does not close (G.1). The exact source decomposition is

```text
O(E+alpha)-E beta
  = EO + E(alpha-beta) + alpha(O-E).                       (G.3)
```

The position commutator controls only `alpha-beta`. More sharply, if `O!=E`, fixing
`E,O,alpha-beta` leaves the left side of (G.3) surjective onto all of `R`; explicit tilts realize
every prescribed sign. Hence every torque-only transition-scale argument is class-completely
dead. Exact balance `E=O=m>0` is the unique exception, where the sharp condition is
`alpha-beta>=-m`.

**Normalized transition observable and reciprocal-concavity target.** The residual has a canonical
dimensionless coordinate

```text
g = beta/O-alpha/E.
```

For `E,O>0`, exact elimination gives

```text
q = -2(1-kappa^2)g,
O(E+alpha)-E beta = EO(1-g).                               (G.4)
```

Thus the negative Xi second curvature forces `g_Xi>0`, while every literal all-angle Suzuki Schur
carrier forces `g<=1`. Any Xi-identifying transition scale must enter the sharp window

```text
0 < g <= 1.                                                (G.5)
```

In safe-shift moments,

```text
g_Xi=(AB+B^2-2AC)/(B(A-B)),
g_Xi<=1  iff  AC-B^2>=0.                                  (G.6)
```

This gate is also exact local response geometry. For the squared-Xi radial field
`G(y)=Re M_0(y)/2`,

```text
(1/G)''(1)=4(B^2-AC)/A^3,
g_Xi<=1  iff  (1/G)''(1)<=0.                              (G.7)
```

So the first nonlinear Suzuki gate is local concavity at the safe shift of the reciprocal radial
logarithmic-derivative response. The generic calculus/Hankel identity is classical; new content is
the literal Suzuki relative-tilt coordinate and its exact prime--Gamma calibration.

**Closed generic positive-resolvent/parity subclass.** Positivity does not force (G.5). For every
`E,O>0` and arbitrary `alpha,beta`, the explicit blocks

```text
R(E,alpha)=[[E,alpha],[alpha,alpha^2/E+1]],
R(O,beta) =[[O,beta ],[beta, beta^2/O+1]]
```

have positive quadratic forms and positive explicit inverses. Their direct sum commutes with
parity. A self-adjoint off-diagonal coordinate-swap position operator reverses parity, and fixed
even/odd basis sources realize exactly `E,O,alpha,beta`. Therefore every source quadruple occurs
inside a positive, invertible, self-adjoint, parity-preserving resolvent model with self-adjoint
parity-reversing position. Combined with fixed-torque surjectivity, generic operator positivity
leaves every sign possible even at fixed `E,O,alpha-beta` off balance.

This is a class-complete no-go for arguments using only positivity, invertibility,
self-adjointness, parity, and abstract position geometry. It does not obstruct Suzuki's concrete
restricted-Weil operator. It proves that the next theorem must use its arithmetic kernel—such as a
source-derived total-positivity, variation-diminishing, or comparison law—not another generic
resolvent estimate.

Even literal exponential sourcing does not repair this class. On the symmetric position spectrum
`{-1,0,1}`, put `C=cosh(1)`, `S=sinh(1)`, `D=C-S`, `P=SC-1`, and

```text
R=diag(1,L,1),       L=2CP/D+1,
u=R(exp(-1),1,exp(1)).
```

This positive diagonal resolvent is invertible, parity preserving, commutes with position, and
has positive inverse; `u` is pointwise positive and solves the literal exponential source
equation. Nevertheless its exact parity moments obey

```text
E=L+2C^2,       O=2S^2,       alpha=beta=2SC,
O(E+alpha)-E beta=-2S(C-S)<0,       g>1.                 (G.8)
```

Thus compact symmetric spectral support, pointwise positivity, exponential sourcing, and even
position-functional-calculus response are class-completely insufficient.

The surviving hard theorem is therefore not “choose a shift with the right torque sign,” nor
“use a positive spectral response.” Finite all-angle Suzuki carriers already force `g<=1`; the
missing source theorem must construct the literal cofinal selected path on which this wall survives
the Xi limit. It is

```text
SUZUKI-SOURCE-SELECTION-AND-TRANSFER:
on one cofinal aperture family, construct a predeclared source-pointed parity profile that
separates reflection before invariant scalarization and is therefore label-sensitive or singular
in every bounded linear realization;
prove either the restricted-Weil bottom-reflection cone (G.0c)
or the exact literal measure-level parity credit/debt budget (G.0o), with the forced
credit--balance--debt turning pattern (G.0p) and stationary mean/variance wall
(G.0r)--(G.0s) whenever both endpoints are sub-Xi;
use its canonical greatest shifts to obtain source-admitted two-angle carriers;
prove their first/second jets and meromorphic quotient converge to the Xi/de Branges target,
thereby transporting the automatic finite wall g<=1 to AC-B^2>=0;
or prove no literal cofinal source family can satisfy this chain.
```

Paper proof and unconditional Lean are in `suzuki-two-jet-shifted-hankel-gate.md` and
`SuzukiTwoJetShiftedHankelGate.lean`,
`SuzukiRelativeTiltReciprocalConcavity.lean`,
`SuzukiSafeShiftResponseChargeCancellation.lean`, and
`SuzukiPositiveResolventParityNoGo.lean`, and
`SuzukiPositiveSpectralResponseNoGo.lean`. The source torque and its resolvent promotion are in

None of this implies that every bounded functional of Suzuki's scalar screw data loses source
orientation: scalar data can recover von Mangoldt coefficients and encode completed divisor.
Neither theorem refutes locally uniform convergence of scalar two-angle carrier. They exclude only
architectures satisfying their stated stable-label, reflected-valuation, finite-Rees, and
post-scalar hypotheses. Singular or relation-valued breadth, beyond-all-orders depth, and
source-selected pre-fusion orientation survive. The updated superselection paper's
admission--separation ledger is decisive here: a target-selected moment/interface can satisfy every
downstream Xi identity while supplying no arithmetic source map. The first-defect cone is therefore
a necessary source test, not an admission theorem, and the singular/pre-fusion factorization is not
proved necessary for Suzuki's concrete scalar finite-interval construction.

**Literal Suzuki screw-kernel and parity carrier.** The published screw source is now an honest
Lean function. Away from prime-power atoms its regular convolution kernel is

`K(t)=exp(t/2)-exp(-5t/2)/(1-exp(-2t))`.

Its sign changes exactly at `t=log ρ_plastic`, where
`ρ_plastic³-ρ_plastic-1=0`. On every atom-free interval Lean proves the distributional identity
`-∫g φ''=∫K φ`, then identifies the two-variable restricted Weil form with `K` paired against
cross-correlation. For every aperture larger than half the sign threshold there are disjoint
nonnegative bumps with strictly positive cross coupling. Consequently no scalar `L²` shift of
the regular form satisfies the first Beurling--Deny inequality; a positivity-preserving-semigroup
repair is impossible in this class.

For an even kernel the full-line form now splits exactly under parity:
`Q_even=T+H`, `Q_odd=T-H`. The Hankel term is the regular convolution pairing minus the finite
prime-power atomic sum `Σ Λ(n)n^{-1/2}c(log n)`, with only `n<exp(2a)` contributing at aperture
`a`. This gives an exact coefficient cocycle and a literal prime Hankel carrier. It closes the
kernel/distribution/parity bookkeeping that external Suzuki formulas motivate, but supplies
neither the source-selected cofinal two-angle limit nor its Xi/de Branges identification.

**Strongest positive survivor and live Route-G core.** Two distinct extension angles give the
meromorphic quotient

```text
W_0/W_1 = (Theta+u_0)/(Theta+u_1),
Theta = (u_0-u_1 W_0/W_1)/(W_0/W_1-1).
```

Common entire gauges cancel and real poles survive.  `SuzukiFiniteApertureHermiteBiehler` proves
this all-angle Livšic/Hermite--Biehler carrier and its normal-family package.  A successor must use
meromorphic/spherical convergence or compact-open convergence away from the limiting pole divisor,
then prove source-selected nondegeneration, uniqueness, and Xi/de Branges identification.  The
coefficient/chord-mass/Pick/Ward work above specifies candidate infrastructure for that repaired
producer. It must now be stated purely as scalar moving-boundary/source convergence: global
deficiency-one phase-block convergence is formally excluded. It is not an unfinished proof of
Suzuki's false one-angle limit, but it remains Suzuki's source-to-Hermite--Biehler route and
therefore keeps Route G open.

**Current verdict.** Route G is open and narrowed. Suzuki's global one-angle holomorphic-gauge
compact-limit program is dead, but the pole-compatible two-angle Livšic/Hermite--Biehler producer
survives. BV/co-Poisson closure and polynomially conditioned finite-Rees reflected sewing are
additional failed provider/source-realization subclasses. Any survivor must now stay in the strict
Xi first-defect cone, construct source-pointed reflection separation before scalarization, prove
cofinal selection through either bottom-reflection fidelity (G.0c) or an interior excursion paying
the exact signed pair budget (G.0o) and necessarily crossing the balance wall (G.0p), retain a
positive ground-leakage floor, satisfy the high-ratio stationary mean/variance wall
(G.0r)--(G.0s), and transport the automatic finite `g<=1` wall through a
source-selected two-angle meromorphic limit to the exact safe-shift Hankel gate. Generic
positive-resolvent parity, positive position-spectral response, or nonlinear postprocessing after
bounded label-blind source transport cannot supply the source projection or convergence.
Full closure requires either an unconditional source-selected two-angle limit and Xi/de Branges
identification or a genuinely class-complete no-go for that meromorphic producer.

**Dead mechanisms.** Every holomorphically gauged one-angle entire compact limit to
`z^2 XiC/XiC'`; legacy ungauged entire compact limit; ordinary Burnol `L2` at critical half-weight;
stable-deficiency phase locking for compact-test graph exhaustion;
polynomially conditioned exact reflected-valuation sewing at physical Suzuki aperture;
every transition-scale argument using only the position-torque scalar away from exact parity

```text
→ zero-adele prime-isotropy relative cocycle and bilateral source Stokes regulator
→ regulator descends to H¹ with retained-class period one
→ strict source-to-resolved-Xi chain map
→ nonzero retained target boundary, annihilated by the next resolved differential
→ reflection-doubled determinant-one and hyperbolic Stokes trivialization
→ positive resolved parallel polarization and explicit fixed-Cartier excess square
→ fixed same-fibre diagonal descent iff boundary triviality
→ boundary triviality iff RH.
```

The primitive promotion is independent of Fisher whitening and grows like `p^(e/2)`. Reflected
exponent sectors have fixed geometric mean, agree only on the critical line, and have exponential
depth mismatch off it. Wold breadth and valuation depth are independent; the first Wold stratum
contains all prime powers, so squarefree projection destroys exactly the depth data needed here.

The full carrier is now literal. `EulerPrimitiveJointDepthHilbertSource.lean` constructs the
prime-depth Hilbert source in its sharp positive chamber and a reciprocal co-source whose pairing
is the complete von Mangoldt channel. `EulerPrimitiveJointDepthKreinXiLanding.lean` supplies a
genuine Hilbert direct sum, an involutive self-adjoint slot-swap fundamental symmetry, and a
Hermitian Krein form with positive diagonal and negative anti-diagonal. The literal source/co-source
pair gives the full complex Laplace channel and completed Xi relative-resolvent trace.
`EulerPrimitiveJointDepthSingularXiLanding.lean` closes the singular landing. Source formation,
Gamma completion, and source-to-Xi transport are therefore not open Route-R premises.

**Constructed nonclassical differential.**
`PrimeDepthBVWoldCurvedSourceDifferential.lean` couples two records from the same literal
prime/depth source:

```text
m_(p,e)=(1-p⁻¹)p⁻ᵉ,
λ_(p,e)=log(p^(e+1)),
θ(i,j)=m_iλ_j-m_jλ_i.
```

On `(2,0),(2,1),(2,2)`, Lean proves additive curvature `-log(2)/8`. Therefore `θ` is not a
vertex coboundary. Equal adjacent clock gaps together with connection difference `log(2)/4` prove
the stronger statement that no function of `λ_j-λ_i` generates it. Exponentiation gives a
sign-sensitive transport, and the complex-linear covariant maps satisfy the exact labelled-square
law

```text
(D1_a D0_a f)(i,j,k)=(U_a(i,j)U_a(j,k)-U_a(i,k))f(k).
```

After the uniform Xi specialization `a_ρ=2 Re(ρ)-1`, reflection inverts transport and the output
retains divisor plus all prime/depth vertices. Witness-square vanishing is equivalent to
`Re(ρ)=1/2`; global differential flatness is equivalent to RH. These are unconditional structure
and endpoint theorems. They do not prove flatness.

**Novelty classification.** Route R is novel at the architecture level in the scoped sense used
by this audit. Euler/Gamma explicit formulas, Krein spaces, discrete covariant derivatives,
curvature as `D²`, and cohomological or flatness aspirations for RH all have classical parents.
What the located literature does not contain is this source chain: two independent records of the
same prime/depth `det₂`--BV class, their unsummed alternating coupling, its proved escape from both
vertex gauges and all clock-difference functions, and the resulting divisor/provenance-resolved
covariant square. That is more than a parameter change inside Hilbert--Pólya, Weil positivity,
Connes trace-formula, or Deninger cohomology routes.

The qualification is essential. The Xi specialization inserts
`a_ρ=2 Re(ρ)-1`; therefore “global flatness iff RH” and “graded-boundary triviality iff RH” are
exact detectors, not by themselves an independent arithmetic proof. Route R becomes a proof route
only at its stated open core: identify the already constructed positive resolved polarization and
retained strict-chain boundary with a fixed same-fibre coefficient-faithful singular Cartier
comparison without using divisor location. Ordinary Cartier exactness,
reflection-determinant triviality, the preserved hyperbolic Stokes pairing, transported
positivity, source Stokes flatness/non-erasure, and source-pure norm-conservative selection have
now been constructed and proved nonselective or conditional on the missing comparison. Thus the
honest labels are
`[CORPUS-NEW ARCHITECTURE] [STRICT RESOLVED SOURCE CHAIN BUILT] [POSITIVE FRAME BUILT]
[SINGULAR CARTIER COMPARISON OPEN]`, not `[RH PROVED]`.

**Closed regular annihilation classes.**

- A positive/orthogonal Gamma correction is impossible: a depth-one central Euler atom exceeds
  `1/5`, while the source zeroth moment is below `29/625 < 1/5`; any matching completion needs
  correction real part below `-96/625`.
- Every nonzero finite or summable nonnegative all-pair Gamma shorting has strictly positive
  cooriented total at nonzero horizontal displacement. A survivor must be sign-indefinite and
  genuinely off-diagonal.
- Every finite noncommutative expression in the log clock and separable incidence is gauge
  conjugate to zero displacement. Finite characteristic polynomials, determinants, and cyclic
  trace moments are horizontally blind.
- For signed scalar modular orbits, unordered `HasSum` convergence at positive real displacement
  forces absolute summability; Bohr uniqueness then forces every coefficient to vanish.
  Distributional scalar completion does not help: canonical cross-prime/depth logarithmic
  frequencies are isolated, and compactly supported test bumps force coefficientwise zero.
- The weighted-Euler oversampling implication has no nonempty positive-strip instance: its
  hypotheses force both `α ≤ β` and `β < α/2`. A useful replacement needs source-derived decay
  beyond the upper carrier wall.
- Common-divisor codiagonal fusion preserves the native hyperbolic signature and hides transverse
  labels in a radical. Infinite unit-residue fusion is not closable in the standard `ℓ²`
  topology. The desired map must act before augmentation, not lift the scalarized packet.
- The actual essential edge and its Fisher model differ by an `ℓ¹` correction throughout the
  Xi strip, but the reflected Fisher pair lies together in one `ℓ²` carrier only for
  `|2 Re(ρ)-1|<1/2`, not for the full unconditional strip `|2 Re(ρ)-1|<1`. A single fixed Hilbert
  ideal therefore cannot carry the required reflected comparison. Any viable completion must use
  variable ideals or an off-diagonal/noncommutative carrier while retaining the `ℓ¹` coefficient
  data.

These no-gos exclude positive cones, finite clock-jet invariants, separable vertex gauges,
unordered scalar sums, scalar distributions after frequency separation, and bounded
label-blind fusion. The constructed BV--Wold connection proves that their surviving
sign-indefinite, relation-valued category is nonempty.

**Exact open core.** Prove from literal arithmetic source structure that the constructed positive
parallel polarization is the fixed same-fibre Cartier polarization on the actual leading Xi
conormal, without assuming divisor location.  The resolved frame now supplies
`H_(a,i)(z)=‖G_(a,i)z‖²`, strict positivity, and
`H_(a,i)(R_(a,ij)z)=H_(a,j)(z)` for every centered rate.  Freezing the source metric at `a=0`
and transporting the clock-normalized vertical conormal gives exact excess
`a² θ(i,j)² |c|²/(λ_i² λ_j²)`.  On the nonzero Xi conormal and witness edge this vanishes exactly
at criticality.  Thus positivity and Stokes flatness separately are no longer missing; their
same-fibre identification is the RH-bearing law. Constructing another nonseparable differential,
equal-grade counterboundary, indefinite trivialization, or transported metric is no longer the
gap.  Nor can identity normalization at infinite Wold depth supply the identification.  In fixed
source coordinates the full relative frame is the exact unipotent gauge
`N(a m_i/lambda_i)`.  For every real `a` it is flat and cycle-trivial and converges to the identity
at every fixed prime as depth tends to infinity, while evaluation at any finite source cell is
injective in `a`.  Every such gauge also fixes the horizontal source subobject and induces the
identity on the vertical quotient, hence acts identically on associated grade.  Its remaining
coordinate is exactly the displacement of the fixed vertical section by `a m_i/lambda_i`.  The
rational-Witt/Koszul source now supplies that distinguished section explicitly: composing its
center section with the normalized Haar orientation section and the scalar reporter lands exactly
at the clock-normalized vertical conormal.  For same-prime cells its Route-R comparison-square
defect is `(-a theta(i,j)c/lambda_j,0)`.  The source-side section is therefore no longer missing.
The rational-adelic zero-isotropy complex now adds a genuine retained ordinary-prime cocycle and a
bilateral source Stokes regulator. The regulator annihilates all relative source boundaries,
descends to genuine `H¹`, and evaluates to one on the retained class. A strict chain map then
lands the class in the resolved actual-Xi complex. Its image is nonzero, retains prime, depth,
divisor, and leading-jet provenance, is exactly a resolved target boundary, and is annihilated by
the next differential. The open law must therefore construct a graded variable-ideal,
coefficient-faithful nonannihilating singular Cartier comparison and prove that this resolved edge
square commutes with the fixed scalar physical fibre on the actual Xi conormal; asymptotic Stokes
uniqueness, bare grade preservation, and existence of a source complex are formally excluded as
remaining gaps.

**Strongest survivor.** Route R contains a complete source-to-Xi Krein producer, a constructed
pre-scalar nonseparable curved differential, exact reflection covariance, and an independently
defined equal-grade graded-`det2` counterboundary whose corrected square vanishes unconditionally
on the actual Xi conormal. Its reflection double has determinant one and preserves its hyperbolic

triviality and hence globally to RH. It also contains a genuine retained rational-adelic
prime-isotropy class with a period-one source Stokes regulator and strict resolved-Xi chain
provenance, and a proof that no fixed Hilbert
ideal covers the full reflected Xi strip. A successful Route-R theorem must now prove the
variable-ideal singular source rigidity identifying the two already constructed polarizations and
the strict resolved boundary with the scalar physical fibre.

**Essential-edge `det2` / BV classification.** The sharp `ell2`/not-`ell1`
strict-preclosure edge now has its canonical diagonal realization. If `a_p` is the literal source
deviation and `r_p=farPrime(p)`, its first two coordinates satisfy

```text
a_p(1)=-p/r_p,
a_p(2)=1/p-p/r_p^2,
a_p(2)-a_p(1)/r_p=1/p.
```

Thus a source-fixed contraction removes the auxiliary Bertrand prime exactly and recovers the
canonical inverse-prime nome. Finite prime-occupation condensation then enumerates the full
reciprocal-positive-integer spectrum `1/N(nu)` exactly once. The resulting diagonal kernel is
Hilbert--Schmidt and not trace class; its quadratic Hilbert--Carleman cocycle is
`t^2*zeta(2)/2`, its local `det2` is `exp(gamma*t)/Gamma(1-t)`, and its polarized quotient is the
regular Wold--Gamma phase.

Prime decimation produces one relative spectral-zeta class

```text
Z_p(s)=(1-p^(-s))*zeta(s).
```

Its actual off-multiple Wold quadratic trace is `Z_p(2)`, while its singular first residue is
`1-p^(-1)`, exactly the Wold first-cell boundary mass and the coefficient of the adjacent BV
continued-source residue. This closes the previously open operator realization and the
regular/singular Wold--Gamma/BV identification in
`PrimeEssentialEdgeOccupationDet2BVRelativeClass.lean`.

The diagonal determinant itself does **not** close Route R: it is an exponential and is proved
nowhere zero.  Its operator does, however, normalize a new equal-grade construction.  For
consecutive source increments `dm(i,j)` and `dl(i,j)`, put crossed parity-diagonal occupation legs

```text
A_a(i,j)=diag((a dm(i,j)/zeta(2))Q, (a dl(i,j)/zeta(2))Q),
B(j,k)=diag(dl(j,k)Q, dm(j,k)Q).
```

Their supertrace product is the consecutive-increment symplectic area

```text
Str(A_a(i,j)B(j,k))
 =a(dm(i,j)dl(j,k)-dl(i,j)dm(j,k))
 =a kappa(i,j,k).
```

Thus the independently defined graded Hilbert--Carleman anomaly
`Anom=exp(-Str(A B))` is the inverse scalar triangle holonomy.  The equal-grade boundary

```text
Boundary_a(f)(i,j,k)
 =U_a(i,j)U_a(j,k)(Anom_a(i,j,k)-1)f(k)
```

satisfies `D1_a D0_a f+Boundary_a(f)=0` unconditionally, including on the actual nonzero leading
Xi conormal.  This is not RH: on the exact three-depth witness, `Boundary=0` iff
`Re(rho)=1/2`, and its global triviality is equivalent to RH. Reflection gives the reciprocal
double `diag(Anom,Anom⁻¹)`. Its determinant and hyperbolic Stokes form trivialize for every rate,
while its positive diagonal descends exactly at rate zero. The frontier has therefore moved from
an unspecified positive Stokes polarization to source rigidity of two explicit positive metrics:
the unconditionally parallel resolved field and the fixed same-fibre Cartier field.

### Non-route coordinate — divisor-fed prime-seam submass

**Endpoint.** `XiPrimeSeamPairEnergySubmass ↔ RH`.

**Unconditional progress.** The degree-one Euler functional-equation coefficient energy
is identified exactly with a horizontal prime-seam weighted norm square. Prime-pair
difference energy divided by reciprocal prime mass converges to twice this quantity, and
the energy controls `(Re(ρ)-1/2)^2 (log 2)^4` on a divisor fibre.

**Classification.** This is not yet a live source route. The predicate quantifies directly
over `XiDivisorSupport`, and its fibrewise content is exactly `Re(ρ)=1/2`; even the
first-coefficient energy remains parameterized by `ρ`.

**Potential promotion condition.** Construct a new source-to-divisor-fibre prime-seam operator
that produces submass growth or zero first-coefficient energy without selecting the Xi divisor.
This would not reopen retired Route F.

**Obstacle.** Existing identities expose and coerce the quantity but do not orient it on
Xi fibres. An outer Euler modulus or prime-seam coupling remains unproduced.

This quotient includes Arakelov power orbits, Hurwitz anchor circuits, Böttcher charges,

No such producer exists. Expected coordinates are often transcendental, so these are not

### Secondary criteria with no new primitive source

The following families reduce to Routes A–S or remain bare equivalences:

- Li coefficient and shifted reciprocal moment criteria;
- Pólya-frequency and consecutive Xi-coefficient Toeplitz-minor criteria;
- Nyman–Beurling/Báez–Duarte closure and its scalar Hardy-cost abstraction;

They remain useful coordinates. None adds an independent source producer.

The exact local Nyman–Beurling scope is smaller than its module commentary: Lean defines the
normalized half-plane kernel and vanishing floor and proves their scalar identities, positivity,
inverse-height upper degradation, and a complementary reciprocal-length inequality. It does not
formalize the fractional-part generators, their Mellin transform, the global closure equivalence,
or an RH connector. Yang's August 2026 localized Friedrichs-angle theorem adds an RH consequence
and unconditional subspace-sum structure, not a distance estimate tending to zero. A Nyman route
therefore remains a classical equivalent closure problem with no new primitive source theorem in
the corpus or located literature.

The local Pólya-frequency scope is likewise smaller than its commentary: Lean proves generic
even-pair/cosh identities, positive finite cosh pairings, and tautological transport of an assumed
`PF ↔ Reality`; it does not formalize the Schoenberg Xi equivalence or literal consecutive
Xi-coefficient minors. `HankelToeplitzWindow` proves an exact factorial window and a generic
order-two log-convex moment inequality, not all Toeplitz minors. Michalowski's July 2026 theorem
proves `D_{r,k} > 0` uniformly for `r ≥ 2` and `k ≥ 10^18 r^3`, but leaves the entire complementary
region—and especially the RH-critical cone `k = O(r)`—open. Its analytic gates use directed-rounding
certificates, so it is not imported under the no-computation rule. The theorem supplies a sharp
external boundary for this secondary criterion, not a source theorem for any live route.

These are not counted as current RH proof chains because no exact source-to-RH handoff is

The literal zeta specialization is closed only for the exact Gamma-jet normal form stated
in `ZetaGammaFieldHypertranscendence.lean`: its denominator, dominant-monomial,
noncancellation, and differential-monomial hypotheses must not be erased. Controlled
digamma quotient valuations and centered coefficient algebraic independence are also now
proved, but neither has an RH handoff.

Dirichlet exponents are now identified exactly as Bohr-hull frequencies under uniform
Dirichlet approximation, closing the exponent-to-spectrum link in the Gamma-field
nonrelation chain. The shift/elimination assembly is now closed at germ level. Rational
germs intertwine rational shift with germ translation, flatness is shift-stable, and each
actual digamma-jet germ satisfies the exact shift-plus-rational-pole law. Polynomial
evaluation into the germ ring is injective: no nonzero differential polynomial in the
digamma jets over `ℂ(s)` is flat on even one vertical line. Universal-to-generic centering,
coefficient-substitution injectivity, and algebraic independence of centered generic
coefficients are proved. The centered coefficient field is a rational function field in
`d-1` independent variables; the associated radical polynomial is irreducible, its root has
the exact expected extension degree, and full symmetric monodromy of the centered equation
survives the even radical base change and every positive odd radical base change; the same
odd-degree law is proved for the uncentered generic polynomial. Every higher digamma
derivative also has its exact vertical leading constant after the natural power
normalization, so normalized jet monomials have explicit nonzero limiting data. Gamma is
transcendental over each finite digamma-jet field in the precise germ-polynomial sense: a
finite polynomial in the Gamma germ with digamma-jet polynomial coefficients vanishes only
coefficientwise. No Xi-source spectral polynomial or analytic handoff exists. The
upper-half-plane germ ring makes every nonzero rational germ a unit, makes flat germs a
rational module, and excludes nonzero rational flat germs.

therefore lists the surviving positive theorem.

### 6.1 Universal finite selectors and local readers

- scalar orbit quotients and unweighted invariant `ℓ²` lifts;
- invariant-fibre “deciders” that merely restate the target.

**Reason.** Explicit no-free-lunch, blindness, completion, gauge, and tail-insertion
theorems preserve all finite observations while changing the unresolved divisor datum.

**Survivor.** Exact sharp resolution/codimension bounds, weighted orbit invariants, and
the conclusion that any viable selector must be infinite, divisor-sensitive, and
source-faithful. For the reverse-Bessel mechanism specifically, the normalized polynomial
has an exact positive Gamma-integral representation, is bounded by the cancelling
exponential, and converges to it at every fixed Euler frequency. The limiting fixed-prime
weights are therefore proportional to `Λ(m)/√m` and are nonsummable: viable growing-order
work must locate and control a moving prime window against the completed source before
passing to the order limit.

### 6.2 Universal Gaussian/CCR/Fock signs

**Dead.**

- universal Gaussian bandwidth log-concavity;
- unrestricted finite full-CCR tuples;
- universal quasi-free Fano nonnegativity without filling;
- compressed-annihilator universal positive defect;
- coherent row/column compensation as a universal sign law;
- all finite annihilation-invariant-subspace sign laws;
- pairwise commutativity as an identification of defect with half the mixed-commutator
  square;
- arbitrary commuting, nilpotent, symmetric-graded tuples as a universal sign producer;
- direct Fock-square repair; cubic/triadic source-incomplete replacements.

**Lean-certified falsifiers.** Degree-five ambient-16 Gaussian positive log-curvature;
finite full-CCR nonexistence; explicit two-mode quasi-free margin `-2`.

**Paper-level retirements.** The compressed-annihilator, row/column compensation,
commuting-tuple, and universal finite invariant-subspace mechanisms are paper-level
falsifications or implications unless a separate Lean declaration is cited.

The proposed complete-Bernstein Gaussian scale law is also dead by a symbolic
equilateral-three-point paper falsifier, but no matching Lean declaration was found; its
status is PAPER-FALSIFIED, not formally certified.

**Survivor.** Rank-one strict concavity, intrinsic-dimension power concavity, invariant
block CCR, sharp filling threshold, rank/phase defect classifications, stable higher-rank
fracture, and the paper-level exact row–column decomposition. The strongest formal
quadratic survivor is the complete graph completion: annihilation invariance and the
determinant law `t^(e+m) det(tI + LᵀL)`.

### 6.3 Generic positivity, moment, and Pick arguments

**Dead.**

- “hypertranscendence implies Hankel positivity”;
- coefficient positivity or complete monotonicity alone;
- axis positivity as a substitute for a Hankel cone;
- generic positive/log-concave Schwartz shape as Fourier positivity;
- direct Pick-kernel positivity at a quadratic boundary contact;
- Schur/Nevanlinna collapse after assuming an RH-equivalent singularity statement;
- one-state universal KMS closure;
- positivity-preserving-semigroup repair by adding any scalar `L²` mass to Suzuki's literal
  regular screw form above the plastic-number threshold aperture;
- Gamma-field asymptotic valuation stopped modulo a vertically flat residual, without
  polynomial-level germ evaluation and shift-stable annihilator assembly.

**Survivor.** Exact counterexamples and boundary-contact signs identify missing literal
source structure; corrected Krein/indefinite kernels remain possible.

### 6.4 Hedenmalm/theta finite-shell mechanisms

**Dead.**

- shellwise sign as the completed sign;
- every fixed finite canonical shell prefix;
- fixed finite endpoint asymptotics;
- order-three positivity as RH closure;
- total-variation domination of the exceptional ground shell at the verified cutoff;
- automatic inward transport from boundary positivity;
- one-particle infinite divisibility of the seam carrier.

**Survivor.** Infinite-tail seam cancellation, global three-point PSD, fourth-jet
positivity, exact higher-shell quadrants, one compact ground defect, and the coupled
completed integral.

### 6.5 Sonin and Suzuki finite completion mechanisms

**Dead.**

- Sonin aperture refinement alone;
- bounded scalar, uniformly bounded operator-family quadratic, fixed-operator, and position-side
  Sonin completions, together with every scalar-additive term eventually below the proved positive
  slope in the typed completed-packet model;
- frequency-reflected and full geometric Burnol log-shell conductors at arbitrary finite-height
  schedules on the canonical source packets;
- second-order Koplienko subtraction that deletes required linear terms;
- legacy Suzuki entire compact limit;
- scalar jump/Herglotz identifications with mismatched coordinates;
- finite signed-frequency repairs;
- local differential corrections;
- unrenormalized cofinal Bianchi mass;
- atomwise finite causal splice;
- raw completed-Mellin positive-domain proposals;
- transfer of symmetric Volterra positivity/sign to the complementary phase coordinate
  under the stated reflection hypotheses;
- rank-one, single-mode, or diagonal spectral positivity as control of projective chord:
  every diagonal chord contribution vanishes.
- scalar-shift Beurling--Deny repair of the literal Suzuki regular kernel: disjoint nonnegative
  bumps have strictly positive cross coupling, while every scalar `L²` correction vanishes on
  disjoint supports.

**Positive survivor, outside retired Route F.** A source-built order-one Sonin operator or a
pre-Gamma renormalization of the Weyl-volume term; transporting a fixed channel by tower dilations
cannot supply it. Separately, the live Suzuki survivors are
meromorphic Suzuki anti-Pick, Herglotz, full-frequency Ward, and projective/Plücker
structures; exact second-jet/safe-shift-Hankel gate; cofinally two-signed literal position torque;
self-adjoint relative phase coordinate, complementary parity sector, genuine spectral-pair
orientation, and independent source-germ-weld route. Torque alone is now excluded off exact parity
balance; local tilt-asymmetry is the missing transition-scale observable.

### 6.6 Möbius/additive cancellation shortcuts

**Dead.**

- fixed-order Gowers savings;
- two-prime positivity by repeated Euler removal;
- ternary slack insertion;
- Lambert ternary lift;
- blockwise LCM/cubical positivity;
- numerical “reciprocal-sum contradiction”.
- fixed finite polynomial moment families in Wold breadth as Möbius-parity selectors;
- off-normal Wold tails or prime-anchor Hall counts presented as central signed cancellation.

**Survivor.** Gamma–Hankel off-diagonal bounded-return target, supercritical LCM boundary

### 6.7 Moving completed-source polynomial parity

**Proposed chain.**

```text
far-right positivity + right-even factorization
→ central source Hankel positivity
→ RH.
```

**Verdict.** PAPER-FALSIFIED. For `P=X`, the second completed moment is positive at the
center, negative at 10, and positive far right, forcing odd-multiplicity crossings and
contradicting even factorization.

**Lean nuance.** Lean proves center positivity and conditionally refutes the factorization
given the negative value at 10; the explicit negative estimate itself is not formalized.

**Survivor.** Four-crossing law and signed crossing structure. Do not erase crossings;
classify them.

### 6.8 Axiomatic and deformation routes

The abstract “positive generalized prime system + Riemann functional equation” lane is
circular-or-false.

**Dead.**

- moving zeros of zeta inside the positive-measure axiom class;
- continuous or nonsummable divisor deformations preserving all axioms;
- intrinsic Landau analysis of the whole source, whose pole at one dominates;
- Hamburger uniqueness as an RH shortcut;
- even/odd Weil splitting as a new lever;
- positive-definiteness tested only in the already zero-free half-plane;
- heat/homotopy flow inside the same domination class.

**Survivor.** Landau deformation rigidity and sharp pole-order budgets classify how a
positive generalized system can deviate. A from-scratch Beurling system remains a
dichotomy about the strength of the axiom class, not a proof route for zeta.

### 6.9 Source algebraicity and representation theatre

**Dead or unsupported.**

- Arakelov integrality without a source producer;
- zero-fed Deninger eigenmodels;
- divisor-preserving dynamics that read the divisor they must control;
- declaring the explicit cofinal source--dagger Bockstein class null-homologous, or requiring the
  literal source residue to annihilate its minimal defect boundary: both are pointwise
  critical-line conditions and globally RH-equivalent;
- abstract witnesses with degenerate inhabitants;
- scalar gluing, orbit shape, ramification quotient, or connected deck charge as
  orientation;
- function-field, scattering, QECC, Böttcher, observer, and physics analogies without a
  typed arithmetic source functor.
- genuinely unbounded closable scalar-current rigging on a dense domain: every closed
  densely defined real scalar functional has full domain, and on a Banach source every
  closed or closable dense scalar functional extends boundedly and continuously;
- invertible linear intertwiners across a trace/character mismatch: one mismatched element
  excludes every invertible intertwiner.
- complete Hochster derived topology plus Stanley–Reisner differential matroid and embedded
  differential field as an orientation selector: arbitrary finite negative residue surgery
  preserves all three while changing the Herglotz chamber.
- residue-programmed diagonal operators as spectral evidence: the canonical `ℓ²` model realizes
  the Gauss-paired logarithmic Schatten residue and exact depth, and its cutoff family escapes every
  fixed positive Schatten class, but the singular values are defined from the target residue and
  no Xi-source operator or RH connector is produced.

**Survivor.** Exact consequence atlases, bounded continuous scalarization, character
equality as a necessary—but not sufficient—compatibility condition, and an exact
topology–orientation product classification. Live rigging must leave the scalar closable
Hilbert category. These structures become RH routes only after a new source construction.
