## 7. Disproof frontier

No unconditional disproof is present. The corpus nevertheless has exact witness-producing
interfaces.

### Direct witness

```text
produce a nontrivial zeta/Xi zero ρ with Re(ρ) ≠ 1/2
→ ¬RH.
```

### Equivalent finite or typed witnesses

| Certificate class | Representative exact interface | Classification |
|---|---|---|
| Completed-flux/Laguerre failure | `completedFluxTaylorCoeff_two_line_re_pos_not_RH` | typed negative certificate |
| Negative completed source jet | `not_riemannHypothesis_of_completedSourceJet_neg` | typed source certificate |
| Prime continuation nonanalyticity | `not_riemannHypothesis_iff_primeContinuationNonanalyticPhaseUniversalCertificate` | exact equivalent certificate |
| Positive Ward endpoint index | `xiEndpointWeightedWardIndex_pos_iff_not_rh` | exact equivalent certificate |
| Nonzero SEM Fitting charge | `not_riemannHypothesis_iff_exists_semHoleFittingCharge_ne_zero` | exact equivalent certificate |
| Heat-Hankel negativity | negative heat form, Turán defect, or trace determinant | finite analytic certificate |
| Shifted-reciprocal negativity | negative reciprocal Hankel quadratic | finite analytic certificate |
| Rectangular/first-clock divergence | cofinal negative rectangular floor or first-clock failure | source-asymptotic certificate |
| Bernstein/source-sector failure | persistent negative mass or sector violation | source-asymptotic certificate |
| Right-half divisor/ladder | nonempty right-half Xi copy and sharp ladder failure | divisor-fed mirror |
| Hurwitz orientation/modulus slope | positive orientation energy or nonzero slope defect | divisor-fed mirror |

### Closest source-level disproof route — growing-order squared-Xi radial jets

For each order `n` and radius `w>0`, the completed source jet `Q_n(w)` has an exact
reverse-Bessel split

```text
Q_n(w) = rational–Gamma contribution
         - positive von-Mangoldt reverse-Bessel contribution.
```

One strict negative value implies `¬RH`. Orders through eighteen are unconditionally
strictly positive, so any witness has `n≥19`. At growing order, normalized reverse-Bessel
weights cancel Euler damping at each fixed prime and converge to nonsummable
`Λ(m)/√m` weights. Least-prime and every fixed finite-prime proof are therefore dead.
The open core is a cancellation-preserving moving-prime-window comparison against the
regularized completed Gamma/polar source, strong enough to make one `Q_n(w)` negative.
Neither corpus nor located literature supplies that comparison.

Every current theorem either derives these from assumed `¬RH` or concludes `¬RH` from
an unproduced certificate. Thus the disproof open core is a concrete certified negative
witness, not a missing logical connector.

## 8. Research hypotheses that would create a leap

These are the first source-level statements not answered by either the formal corpus or the
primary literature audited in Section 9 and the route-by-route companion paper.

### Terminal coordinates, not research hypotheses

Route A's first-clock floor and Bohr asymptote, Route M's cofinal reader flatness, retired Route
J's unrestricted residual capacity, Route P's completed all-order moment condition, and every
criterion stated directly on Xi divisor support are exact RH coordinates. Proving another
equivalent wrapper around one of them does not advance source acquisition.

### Tier 1 — shortest live chains

1. **Route R singular Cartier comparison.** Construct a graded variable-ideal,
   coefficient-faithful comparison carrying the retained rational-adelic prime-isotropy boundary
   from the positive resolved frame to the fixed same-fibre physical Xi conormal. The source
   complex, strict resolved chain map, period-one source Stokes regulator, transported positive
   polarization, Stokes flatness, and source-pure norm-conservative selector already exist; the
   comparison must not assume the equivalent critical-line condition.
2. **Post-J corpus-native successor `[NEW ROUTE IF INSTANTIATED]`.** Construct before good/bad
   projection a literal source-defined principal-relative current whose incidence is not a
   vertex-gauge coboundary, and derive an arithmetic null-homotopy forcing its Burnol/Deninger
   reflection discriminant to vanish. If it is not the existing Route-R current, it must receive
   separate route ownership. Backlog card:
   `../0-backlog/post-j-preprojection-principal-relative-current.md`.
3. **Route L source-null-homology.** Prove that the explicitly measured physical paracyclic defect
   is null-homologous in the literal arithmetic cyclic source, equivalently supply the missing
   singular boundary compatibility in the Green `tensor BV` square. The ordered physical
   comparison, residue `(delta_n(rho)-1)c_rho`, and trace-one prime detector already exist and may
   not be erased.
4. **CCM-FU prelimit noncompact incidence.** Construct the moving contractive family carrying the
   joined lower Fisher corner and physical all-jet horizon through Gamma/co-Poisson/Hodge descent
   to the continued Xi Ward column while preserving its exact four-unit energy.
5. **Route E source-pointed physical Hall relation.** Give an exact source-predeclared formula for
   `R_X` on the squarefree central normal Wold face, then prove normalized load/co-degree, full
   interval Hall expansion, co-moving friable/one-large-prime flux, and square-root Gibbs cost for
   that fixed relation.
6. **Route C1 singular Xi--Weil boundary.** Either construct the source-derived Hedenmalm
   common-domain/boundary transgression which realizes the already identified Weil form faithfully
   without assuming its sign, or construct the bivariant/
   intersection trace pairing on the exact closed CCM quotient, prove its Hodge sign, and identify
   it with the Weil pairing, without assuming divisor location.
7. **Route D2 prime--archimedean ceiling.** Derive any finite uniform high-frequency ceiling from
   the literal source above the proved low-frequency reconstruction.
8. **Route G two-angle meromorphic producer.** Prove pole-compatible source-selected
   nondegeneration, full jump Pick/Ward sign, and Xi/de Branges identification for the surviving
   two-angle Livšic/Hermite--Biehler family. The boundary-theta cumulant bridge is closed; its
   remaining local subgate is one sharp skewness budget.
9. **Route N prime-normal domain theorem.** Prove the uniform source-domain sub-log-square
   estimate on the closed theta Grushin domain with the full Jordan tower.
10. **Route O whole-ground profile theorem.** Hostile-audit and Lean-certify the concrete
   radial-PSWF realization of the provisionally paper-closed exterior seed law, then prove a
   source-derived low-energy decomposition into prolate, translated two-edge, prime-phase, and
   coercive-bulk channels. Prove profile exhaustion, intact polar-prime cross-term control, and
   relative bulk coercivity before attempting the RH-bearing boundary orientation/floor theorem.
11. **Route P compact moment carrier.** Construct the literal completed-seam compact positive
    Thorin/Markov carrier; Route K's all-order marked-prime extension lands here.
12. **Route Q carrier gate or saturation theorem.** Decide the forced source inequality
    `normalizedPrimeArchMoment 2 < normalizedPrimeArchMoment 1/81` for the unique selected
    `alpha_*>4` carrier; if it survives, prove all-degree matching, or instead prove the boundary
    Jensen/jet saturation inequality directly.

### Tier 2 — new source-to-operator constructions

13. **Order-one Sonin successor.** Build an arithmetic packet-diagonal operator of microlocal
    order at least one, or remove the Sonin Weyl-volume term before Gamma attachment. This is a
    new route exposed by retired Route F, not a repair inside it.
14. **All-degree prime--archimedean form.** Construct source-built Hankel positivity and
    reconstruction beyond Route D's retired finite mechanism.
15. **Deninger relevant-complex realization.** Extend the now-formalized punctured-plane primitive
    decomposition and one-dimensional `c·dlog z+dH` cohomology line to the instantiated
    rational-Witt arithmetic model with the literal relevant coefficient complex, nuclear
    topology, cup/trace/Hodge maps, and a source-faithful comparison whose closed middle boundary
    is the arithmetic kernel. This is upstream of, and must feed, Route L's exact Stokes theorem.
16. **Critical Plücker continuation.** Carry the proved finite-prime/Euler-boundary normal-cone
    diagonal from `Re(s)>1` to critical Möbius data without importing the Mertens estimate.
17. **Singular Hedenmalm completion.** Construct the source-defined closed relation and prove
    complete Xi--Weil positivity on it, not merely a bounded regular extension.
18. **Direct cofinal Connes comparison.** Obtain the restricted-Weil ground inequality from the
    adelic source independently of the finite determinant's RH-equivalent seed comparison.

### Tier 3 — enabling structure

19. Non-diagonal inter-scale KMS inequality neutralizing persistent bad layers.
20. Contractive or variation-diminishing Möbius inversion of Gamma occupation.
21. Source-stable OPUC terminal identity.
22. Burnol co-Poisson scalar Berezin sign or parity-block flatness.
23. Source determinant-line no-first-crossing theorem retaining negative index.
24. Viable relation-valued or nonclosable post-source rigging that remains label-sensitive.
25. Actual two-variable HT/SR realization with rank-one period group and a typed RH handoff.
26. A theorem converting Dirichlet/Gamma differential nonrelation into zero-location
    information.
27. Lindelöf second-spacing plus across-class cancellation with an exact source-to-RH connector.
28. A cancellation-preserving moving-prime-window asymptotic for growing reverse-Bessel
    squared-Xi jets, strong enough to orient one order `n≥19`.
29. Uniform positivity of consecutive Xi-coefficient Toeplitz minors in
    `k < 10^18 r^3`, especially `k=O(r)`, without numerical certification.

### Disproof leap

30. A rigorously certified off-line zero or any equivalent negative finite certificate.

### Internal publication-package reconciliation

Publication-stage location is not evidence of a valid RH connector. The present claim audit gives
the following status.

- `riemann-arithmetic-source-dagger-route` now distinguishes the failed old product reporter from
  the literal degree-one cyclic-bar source object. `MobiusThetaSourceDaggerMinimalBoundaryLaw.lean`
  constructs its rotation-invariant trace, trace-one ordinary-prime atom, ordered pre-divisor
  physical comparison, filtered Xi-jet landing, residue detector, and exact paracyclic
  transgression. Its formal claim map explicitly leaves physical source-null-homology unproved.
  The independent prime-isotropy relative complex and period-one Stokes regulator supply strict
  resolved provenance but still do not prove that homology statement or scalar Cartier descent.
- `real-corona-isotropy-clifford-transgression-law` and the exact nonunital CCM modules establish
  recipient nontriviality, Real-parity obstruction, and the canonical `iL` survivor. They advance
  C1-CCM's carrier, not its missing trace-pairing positivity.
- `real-corona-quaternionic-cyclic` constructs a canonical Hardy-corona upper/lower projector,
  quaternionic Real structure, and graded cyclic survivor on the divisor-defined Hardy quotient.
  It is unconditional structure but not a source proof route: the inner factor is built from the
  completed divisor, and crossed-product descent makes ordinary cyclic degree zero trace-blind.
- `riemann-boundary-completeness-law` and `riemann-hellinger-hodge-green-descent` prove universal
  kernel-pair, first-loss, resolved-grade, and polarized-conservativity laws and instantiate a broad
  completed Riemann source. They sharply constrain every route but do not produce the stated
  source-derived support exclusion or equal-grade transverse relation. They are frontier
  classification papers, not additional independent proof routes.
- `riemann-oriented-source-admission`, `riemann-source-orientation-transgression`, and
  `source-horizon-riemann-divisor` sharpen the same classification in three complementary
  directions: relative admission spectra locate the exact class lost by a domain change; source
  cohomology isolates one orientation line plus the Green primitive; and the complete prime-normal
  horizon proves finite-shell faithfulness can coexist with cofinal collapse and maximal
  multivalued closure. Their combined impact is the parallel polarized-source atlas in Section 1:
  regular serial completion cannot repair a lost horizontal class, but several nonisomorphic
  source charts may close RH if their observation radicals intersect trivially. They do not supply
  any chart's missing singular/cofinal arithmetic admission theorem.
- `riemann-relation-kernel-transgression` constructs a nonzero divisor-independent
  relation-kernel calibrator and a literal Meyer--Poisson boundary regulator. Its sole open bridge
  is the coefficient-faithful comparison
  `Trans(Green provenance at ρ)=(2 Re(ρ)-1)τ_p`. This is a concrete Route-L comparison target,
  not a closed connector and not a separate route after quotienting by first unresolved premise.
- `airy-negative-local-fourier-source`, `airy-tower-zero-loop-stokes-rigidity`, and
  `stokes-cartan-lorentz-capacity-law` strengthen the irregular/Stokes parent geometry available
  to Route R, but none contains a literal ordinary-prime/depth-to-Xi comparison.
- `two-sided-singular-gelfand-defects` proves that countably many injective dense-range,
  noncoercive observers may retain large common closed defects. It blocks singularity or
  noncoercivity alone from serving as Route R's missing rigidity; a source-faithful arithmetic
  invariant is indispensable.

## 9. Literature reconciliation

Current primary literature distinguishes new finite or structural advances from proof
completion.

- Connes and van Suijlekom prove real zeros for the Fourier transform of an isolated
  simple even ground state of a lower-bounded convolution operator. This validates a
  spectral mechanism but does not identify a convergent Xi ground state:
  [Quadratic Forms, Real Zeros and Echoes of the Spectral Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23257).
- Connes's survey obtains real finite approximants and explicitly leaves finite-to-infinite
  Euler-product convergence as the prospective proof step:
  [The Riemann Hypothesis: Past, Present and a Letter Through Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04022).
- Connes--Consani--Marcolli construct the cyclic-homology cokernel of the adelic class-space
  restriction and identify RH with positivity of its trace pairing. The exact recipient is now
  represented in the corpus, but the bivariant/intersection pairing and its Weil identification
  are not:
  [The Weil proof and the geometry of the adeles class space](https://arxiv.org/abs/math/0703392).
- Connes–Consani–Moscovici report striking finite spectral convergence; they state that a
  rigorous proof of convergence would establish RH:
  [Zeta Spectral Triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22755).
- Hedenmalm constructs the theta boundary-value eigenproblem and a formal self-adjoint pair. The
  corpus now identifies its canonical source form with the polarized Weil distribution and rules
  out faithful full-source ordinary-`L2` Hilbert-Gram closure; the open problem is a source-derived
  singular common domain/boundary transgression, not an unspecified positive Hilbert realization:
  [Spectral interpretation of Riemann zeta zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17494).
- Suzuki constructs finite self-adjoint extensions and conjectures the limiting
  self-adjoint operator. The infinite/full-frequency identification remains open:
  [Weil's quadratic form via the screw function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09096).
- The July 2026 finite-element realization of Suzuki's operator proves an archimedean
  Mellin head coefficient but classifies its spectral, ground-state, characteristic-zero,
  and critical-line observations as numerical and universal rather than new arithmetic;
  it supplies no unconditional finite-to-Xi limit:
  [A Numerical Realization of Suzuki's Weil-Quadratic-Form Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24830).
- The finite Guinand–Weil dictionary gives exact finite zero sums and an explicit
  archimedean tail budget, while expressly making no RH claim:
  [A finite Guinand-Weil dictionary and archimedean tail order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02828).
- Liflandsky's discrete Möbius Laplace bound implies RH, while the converse needs extra
  zero hypotheses. The critical bound remains an input:
  [Explicit formula for the discrete Laplace transform of the Möbius function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09797).
- Verjovsky proves that subpolynomial shrinking-arc local moments of Möbius Fourier
  polynomials, at arbitrarily high finite orders, are equivalent to RH and gives an
  unconditional moment-to-point inequality recovering `M(N)`. No local-moment bound is
  proved, so the Route E source estimate is unchanged:
  [Local Moments of Möbius Fourier Polynomials and the Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25002).
- The theta-infinity paper proves zero-free consequences conditional on arbitrary-length
  mollified moment bounds; it does not prove those bounds:
  [The θ=∞ Conjecture and RH for Automorphic L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24363).
- The 2026 Hardy-space and Colombeau papers give closure/generalized-function criteria,
  not the missing source estimates:
  [Hardy space approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16097) and
  [Colombeau–Beurling criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22562).
- Yang proves RH-implied zero Friedrichs angles for localized Nyman–Beurling subspace pairs and
  unconditional strict subspace-sum inclusions. These sharpen the Hilbert geometry but do not
  provide the missing approximation estimate:
  [A Friedrichs angle between the Nyman–Beurling spaces and the Riemann hypothesis](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jmaa.2026.130494).
- Michalowski proves consecutive Xi-coefficient Toeplitz-minor positivity in the explicit
  uniform tail wedge `k ≥ 10^18 r^3`; the paper itself leaves `k < 10^18 r^3`, particularly
  `k = O(r)`, open and makes no RH claim. Its rigorous proof uses directed-rounding
  certificates, precluding import under the no-computation rule:
  [An explicit uniform cubic wedge for consecutive Toeplitz minors of the Riemann ξ-coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16795).
- Gaber's July preprint is not a valid SOTA input. Its claimed RH-conditional `O(x)` error
  for the summatory Euler totient at `ℓ=1` contradicts Montgomery's unconditional
  `Ω±(x sqrt(log log x))` theorem, so no result from it is imported:
  [Euler's ℓ-totients and Riemann hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26114),
  [Fluctuations in the mean of Euler's phi function](https://doi.org/10.1007/BF02837826).
- The Salem note is explicitly an equivalence:
  [A new equivalence by means of the Salem integral equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15396).
- Polson's revised Thorin paper establishes the probabilistic/Hadamard framework, not a
  source-constructed critical-seam carrier:
  [Riemann, Thorin, van Dantzig Pairs, Wald Couples and Hadamard Factorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/1804.10043).

The cited primary sources close no Tier-1 or Tier-2 open core above.

## 10. Priority and research policy

### Highest expected leverage

1. Route R graded variable-ideal singular Cartier comparison from the strict rational-adelic
   prime-isotropy boundary and positive resolved frame to the fixed physical Xi fibre;
2. C1-CCM bivariant/intersection trace pairing, Hodge sign, and Weil-form identification on the
   now-exact closed nonunital strong cyclic quotient;
3. Route L singular source-to-physical specialization through the exact Burnol/Deninger radial
   obstruction;
4. post-J pre-projection principal-relative current as a separately owned route, unless its exact
   construction is the existing Route-R source;
5. Route E explicit central physical relation and its joint interval-Hall/co-moving-flux theorem;
6. Hedenmalm singular completion or prime-normal domain theorem;
7. corrected pole-compatible two-angle Suzuki source-selected full jump kernel;
8. a literal prime--archimedean high-frequency ceiling.

These routes begin with literal source and have short, formally closed tails to RH.

### Valuable but longer-horizon construction programs

- arithmetic Deninger relevant-complex realization feeding Route L;
- Route O full-ground profile exhaustion/reduced-resolvent control, after hostile audit and Lean
  certification of the provisionally paper-closed exterior prolate seed law;
- compact Thorin/Markov carrier.

### Do not spend cycles on

- new names for RH-equivalent divisor coordinates;
- first-clock tail/Bohr restatements, fixed physical-phase intertwiners, or positive
  source-faithful Markov mixers without a new source-first invariant;
- finite local selectors;
- shellwise or finite-prefix positivity as completed positivity;
- abstract zero-fed spectral models;
- generic HT, positivity, log-concavity, or moment arguments without literal source;
- positive/nonnegative Gamma shorting, finite clock-jet spectra, separable vertex-gauge
  incidence, unordered scalar orbit sums, or post-separation scalar distributions for Route R;
- fixed finite Wold moments, off-normal breadth tails, or prime-anchor counting presented as the
  missing Route E cancellation;
- absolute covariant curvature, generic mapping-cone exactness, or finite top-boundary
  non-erasure presented as Route L's source Stokes theorem;
- unrenormalized Julia energy, post hoc diagonal subtraction, bounded polarizations, or
  separable cycle holonomy presented as an unfinished Route-J current;
- Connes/prolate determinant transfers that do not first prove a new literal cofinal Weil-floor
  inequality;
- legacy Suzuki entire-limit, any finite-height geometric Sonin/Burnol completion, or
  moving-polynomial even factorization;
- ordinary, logarithmic-boundary, compact-seed, or generic property-S repackagings of Route G;
- lexical HT/RH crossmatches without exact imports and identifier use.

## 11. Final frontier statement

RH is neither proved nor disproved. The corpus has formed the positive Euler activation source,
its complete breadth/depth resolution, primitive von-Mangoldt current, Gamma completion, and
regular and singular source-to-Xi landings. It has also proved that many natural observations lose
the only RH-bearing coordinate:

```text
bounded label-blind codiagonal       → retains total mass, kills source radical
positive reflection regularization  → retains squared curvature, loses orientation
separable prime-depth incidence      → vertex-gauge coboundary, cycle invariants blind
finite clock-jet spectral invariant  → gauge-conjugate to zero displacement
scalar frequency separation          → coefficientwise uniqueness, no nonzero shorting
absolute covariant curvature         → flat after canonical countertransport.
```

The first unresolved theorem is therefore route-dependent but consistently pre-scalar:

- Route R has a source-derived, sign-indefinite, nonseparable arithmetic differential before
  vertex gauge and frequency separation, plus a genuine rational-adelic relative complex,
  retained ordinary-prime `H1` class, normalized source Stokes period, and strict resolved-Xi
  chain map with unconditional `D1 D0=0`.  The strict image is a target boundary.  It now needs a
  graded variable-ideal, coefficient-faithful singular Cartier comparison retaining that chosen
  Xi primitive and the graded `ell1` subobject and identifying the positive resolved frame with
  the fixed physical fibre; an ordinary cohomology chain map or fixed Hilbert ideal cannot do so.
- Retired Route J has no open premise. A different pre-projection principal-relative current is a
  corpus-native new-route candidate; it belongs to Route R only if its literal source and handoff
  are the existing Route-R construction.
- Route L needs source-null-homology of the measured physical paracyclic defect, equivalently the
  missing singular boundary compatibility in its Green--BV square. Completed Green source and
  primitive, BV boundary, resolved complex, strict Route-R chain provenance, ordered pre-divisor
  physical comparison, exact residue, trace-one prime detector, Burnol/Deninger reader, and
  coefficient-faithful landing exist. The missing law must make that fixed reader boundary-blind
  without erasing its detector; failure is exactly the RH-bearing radial coefficient.
- The unlettered CCM-FU lane needs a prelimit cofinal noncompact incidence from the joined lower
  Fisher corner and admitted physical jet horizon to the continued Xi Ward column. Its exact
  four-unit source budget, weak/non-strong critical escape, compact-synthesis no-go, and all-jet
  horizon admission are Lean-closed.
- Route E needs an exact source-predeclared physical relation `R_X`, then its joint central
  interval-Hall/co-moving-cutoff theorem. Sieve counts, coprime opposite-sign supply, prime-anchor
  capacity, off-normal tails, finite moments, and telescoping polynomial seams do not define that
  adjacency or orient the squarefree breadth face.
- Route C1 now has an exact nonzero closed CCM class-space recipient with descended Lefschetz and
  Real/Clifford structure. Its Hedenmalm source Weil form, folded Dirac defect, and positive Borel
  holonomy are also identified. It still needs either the singular source-derived Hedenmalm
  common-domain/boundary transgression or the CCM bivariant/intersection trace pairing, Hodge sign,
  and Weil identification.
- Route O's aperture-independent radial comparison defect, simple-pole/mass bounds, and `O(1/c)`
  Volterra exponent are Lean-closed. Its complete exterior prolate seed law is provisionally
  paper-closed and awaits concrete radial-PSWF Lean certification. The scientific gap is now
  profile exhaustion/reduced-resolvent control of the whole restricted-Weil ground space, including
  the prime-phase channel, followed by the RH-bearing boundary orientation or floor theorem.
- Routes D2, G, N, P, and Q retain the distinct open cores stated in Section 8. Route G's
  boundary-theta cumulant bridge is closed up to one sharp skewness budget; Route Q's selected
  carrier is now confined to `(0,1/81)` and faces the explicit source ratio gate `m_2<m_1/81`.

Route A is terminalized as an exact RH coordinate after phase-natural rigidity and barycentric
completeness. Routes B, C2, D, F, G2, H, I, J, and K are retired as independent mechanisms. Route M
is terminalized at a source-faithful reader whose remaining flatness is RH. Route K's strongest
quadratic/quartic marked-prime survivor transfers to Route P at all orders. Retired routes can
motivate new mechanisms only through their recorded positive survivors; they contain no
unfinished connector whose completion would prove RH.

The external literature audited through 4 August 2026 contributes useful finite spectral,
sieve, Hedenmalm, Suzuki, Möbius-moment, and Deninger/Connes infrastructure. Where a theorem was
both unconditional and useful, the corpus has formalized or scheduled its precise reusable
content. No located primary source supplies Route R's singular Cartier comparison, C1-CCM's
positive bivariant/intersection pairing on the exact recipient, the post-J pre-projection current,
Route L's physical source-null-homology, CCM-FU's prelimit noncompact Ward incidence, Route E's
explicit physical `R_X` and central-Hall/co-moving-flux theorem, or another live route's first open
premise.

The disproof map is equally closed at the connector level: one certified off-line Xi zero or an
equivalent finite negative witness suffices, but none is present. Growing-order squared-Xi radial
jets remain the most explicit source-level candidate; orders through eighteen are positive and
the moving-prime comparison for `n≥19` is open.

> **Current frontier:** source, resolved landing, positive transported geometry, strict
> prime-isotropy provenance, literal physical comparison, and nonzero source detectors are largely
> built. What is not built is an arithmetic law forcing the retained reflection-odd horizontal
> class to vanish before scalarization. Route R asks for variable-ideal singular Cartier descent;
> Route L for physical source-null-homology; CCM-FU for a moving noncompact four-unit Ward
> incidence; C1-CCM for the positive intersection pairing. These are distinct realizations of one
> polarized-conservativity problem, not interchangeable routine lemmas.
